FILE: megakernel/server/mk_server.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""OpenAI-compatible chat server for the Qwen3.8-27B megakernel (a drop-in for the parts of
llama-server's API that open-jet uses).

  python3 server/mk_server.py -m model.gguf --port 18080 [--ctx 32768] [--draft 4]

POST /v1/chat/completions (streaming or not): the request is rendered with the chat template stored
in the GGUF (enable_thinking / preserve_thinking / reasoning_effort via chat_template_kwargs or
top-level fields; high maps to xhigh and none disables thinking). Tools arrive via `tools`;
reasoning is returned as `reasoning_content`, Qwen's XML tool calls as structured
`tool_calls`.  Sampling: temperature, top_p, top_k (<= 32), min_p, seed.  Successive requests of a
conversation reuse the processed tokens: the new prompt is matched against the sequence so far and
only the rest is prefilled (rewinding to the end of the previous prompt if it diverges after that).
GET /health, GET /v1/models.  One request at a time.
"""
import argparse
import codecs
import ctypes
import json
import math
import os
import random
import re
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

import jinja2
from jinja2.sandbox import ImmutableSandboxedEnvironment

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
TOPK_MAX = 32


def log(*a):
    print("[mk-server]", *a, file=sys.stderr, flush=True)


def model_mtp_layers(lib, model):
    """Use the host-only CUDA loader schema check before choosing a decoder."""
    L = ctypes.CDLL(lib)
    if not hasattr(L, 'mk_model_mtp_layers'):
        raise RuntimeError('rebuild libqwen38mk.so for automatic MTP detection, or specify --draft explicitly')
    L.mk_model_mtp_layers.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p]
    L.mk_model_mtp_layers.restype = ctypes.c_int
    L.mk_last_error.restype = ctypes.c_char_p
    mtp = L.mk_model_mtp_layers(os.fsencode(model))
    if mtp < 0:
        raise RuntimeError(L.mk_last_error().decode())
    return mtp


class Engine:
    """ctypes binding of capi.cpp"""

    def __init__(self, lib, model, ctx, n_draft, draft_vocab, kv_mode=-1, draft_model=None):
        L = self.L = ctypes.CDLL(lib)
        vp, ip, cp = ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int), ctypes.c_char_p
        sig = {
            "mk_new": (vp, [cp, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, cp, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_last_error": (cp, []),
            "mk_chat_template": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, cp, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_tokenize": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, cp, ctypes.c_int, ip, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_piece": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, ctypes.c_int, cp, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_is_eog": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_set_sampling": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, ctypes.c_float, ctypes.c_float, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_float,
                                               ctypes.c_ulonglong]),
            "mk_prefill": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, ip, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_step": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, ip, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_reset": (None, [vp]),
            "mk_truncate": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_sequence": (ctypes.c_int, [vp, ip, ctypes.c_int]),
            "mk_committed": (ctypes.c_int, [vp]),
            "mk_checkpoint_pos": (ctypes.c_int, [vp]),
            "mk_rewind_to_checkpoint": (ctypes.c_int, [vp]),
        }
        for name, (res, args) in sig.items():
            f = getattr(L, name)
            f.restype, f.argtypes = res, args
        if draft_model:
            L.mk_new_dflash.restype=vp
            L.mk_new_dflash.argtypes=[cp,cp,ctypes.c_int,ctypes.c_int,ctypes.c_int]
            self.h=L.mk_new_dflash(model.encode(),draft_model.encode(),ctx,n_draft,kv_mode)
        else:
            self.h = L.mk_new(model.encode(), ctx, n_draft, draft_vocab.encode() if draft_vocab else None, kv_mode)
        if not self.h:
            raise RuntimeError(L.mk_last_error().decode())
        self.ctx = ctx
        self._pieces = {}

    def _err(self, what):
        return RuntimeError(f"{what}: {self.L.mk_last_error().decode()}")

    def chat_template(self):
        n = self.L.mk_chat_template(self.h, None, 0)
        buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(n + 1)
        self.L.mk_chat_template(self.h, buf, n)
        return buf.raw[:n].decode()

    def tokenize(self, text):
        b = text.encode()
        cap = len(b) + 16
        out = (ctypes.c_int * cap)()
        n = self.L.mk_tokenize(self.h, b, len(b), out, cap)
        if n < 0:
            cap = -n
            out = (ctypes.c_int * cap)()
            n = self.L.mk_tokenize(self.h, b, len(b), out, cap)
        return list(out[:n])

    def piece(self, tok):
        p = self._pieces.get(tok)
        if p is None:
            buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(256)
            n = self.L.mk_piece(self.h, tok, buf, 256)
            p = self._pieces[tok] = buf.raw[:max(n, 0)]
        return p

    def is_eog(self, tok):
        return bool(self.L.mk_is_eog(self.h, tok))

    def set_sampling(self, temp, top_p, top_k, min_p, seed):
        if self.L.mk_set_sampling(self.h, temp, top_p, top_k, min_p, seed) < 0:
            raise self._err("sampling")

    def prefill(self, toks):
        arr = (ctypes.c_int * len(toks))(*toks)
        r = self.L.mk_prefill(self.h, arr, len(toks))
        if r < 0:
            raise self._err("prefill")
        return r

    def step(self, remaining=None):
        out = (ctypes.c_int * 8)()
        cap = 8 if remaining is None or os.environ.get("MK_DFLASH_BUDGET") == "0" else min(8, max(1, remaining))
        n = self.L.mk_step(self.h, out, cap)
        if n < 0:
            raise self._err("decode")
        return list(out[:n])

    def reset(self):
        self.L.mk_reset(self.h)

    def truncate(self, n):
        if self.L.mk_truncate(self.h, n) < 0:
            raise self._err("truncate")

    def sequence(self):
        n = self.L.mk_sequence(self.h, None, 0)
        out = (ctypes.c_int * max(n, 1))()
        self.L.mk_sequence(self.h, out, n)
        return list(out[:n])

    def committed(self):
        return self.L.mk_committed(self.h)

    def checkpoint_pos(self):
        return self.L.mk_checkpoint_pos(self.h)

    def rewind_to_checkpoint(self):
        return self.L.mk_rewind_to_checkpoint(self.h) == 0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# chat template and output parsing


def make_template(src):
    env = ImmutableSandboxedEnvironment(trim_blocks=True, lstrip_blocks=True, extensions=["jinja2.ext.loopcontrols"])

    def raise_exception(msg):
        raise jinja2.exceptions.TemplateError(msg)

    env.globals["raise_exception"] = raise_exception
    env.filters["tojson"] = lambda x, indent=None, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=False: json.dumps(
        x, indent=indent, ensure_ascii=ensure_ascii, sort_keys=sort_keys)
    return env.from_string(src)


class BadRequest(Exception):
    pass


def normalize_messages(messages):
    out = []
    for m in messages:
        m = dict(m)
        content = m.get("content")
        if isinstance(content, list):
            for part in content:
                if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") not in (None, "text"):
                    raise BadRequest(f"unsupported content part type {part.get('type')!r} (text only)")
        if content is None:
            m["content"] = ""
        if m.get("role") == "assistant" and m.get("tool_calls"):
            calls = []
            for tc in m["tool_calls"]:
                tc = dict(tc)
                fn = dict(tc.get("function") or {})
                args = fn.get("arguments")
                if isinstance(args, str):
                    try:
                        args = json.loads(args) if args.strip() else {}
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        args = {}
                fn["arguments"] = args if isinstance(args, dict) else {}
                tc["function"] = fn
                calls.append(tc)
            m["tool_calls"] = calls
        out.append(m)
    return out


TOOL_CALL_RE = re.compile(r"<tool_call>\s*<function=([^>\n]+)>(.*?)(?:</function>\s*(?:</tool_call>|$)|$)", re.S)
PARAM_RE = re.compile(r"<parameter=([^>\n]+)>\n?(.*?)\n?</parameter>", re.S)


def parse_tool_calls(text, tools):
    schemas = {}
    for t in tools or []:
        fn = t.get("function", t)
        schemas[fn.get("name")] = (fn.get("parameters") or {}).get("properties") or {}
    calls = []
    for m in TOOL_CALL_RE.finditer(text):
        name = m.group(1).strip()
        props = schemas.get(name, {})
        args = {}
        for p in PARAM_RE.finditer(m.group(2)):
            key, val = p.group(1).strip(), p.group(2)
            typ = (props.get(key) or {}).get("type")
            if typ == "string" or (typ is None and not val.strip()[:1] in "[{0123456789-tfn"):
                args[key] = val
            else:
                try:
                    args[key] = json.loads(val)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    args[key] = val
        calls.append({"id": "call_" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:24], "type": "function",
                      "function": {"name": name, "arguments": json.dumps(args, ensure_ascii=False)}})
    return calls


class OutputParser:
    """Splits the generated text into reasoning (before </think>), content and tool-call markup."""

    def __init__(self, thinking, tools):
        self.mode = "reasoning" if thinking else "content_start"
        self.tools = tools
        self.buf = ""
        self.tool_text = ""

    def feed(self, text, final=False):
        """returns [(kind, text)] with kind in {reasoning, content}"""
        out = []
        self.buf += text
        while True:
            if self.mode == "reasoning":
                i = self.buf.find("</think>")
                if i >= 0:
                    out.append(("reasoning", self.buf[:i]))
                    self.buf = self.buf[i + len("</think>"):]
                    self.mode = "content_start"
                    continue
                keep = 0 if final else len("</think>") - 1
                if len(self.buf) > keep:
                    out.append(("reasoning", self.buf[:len(self.buf) - keep]))
                    self.buf = self.buf[len(self.buf) - keep:]
                break
            if self.mode == "content_start":
                self.buf = self.buf.lstrip()
                if not self.buf:
                    break
                self.mode = "content"
                continue
            if self.mode == "content":
                if self.tools:
                    i = self.buf.find("<tool_call>")
                    if i >= 0:
                        out.append(("content", self.buf[:i].rstrip()))
                        self.tool_text = self.buf[i:]
                        self.buf = ""
                        self.mode = "tools"
                        break
                    keep = 0 if final else len("<tool_call>") - 1
                else:
                    keep = 0
                if final:
                    out.append(("content", self.buf.rstrip()))
                    self.buf = ""
                elif len(self.buf) > keep:
                    # trailing whitespace waits for more text: it is dropped before a tool call or the end
                    seg = self.buf[:len(self.buf) - keep]
                    head = seg.rstrip()
                    out.append(("content", head))
                    self.buf = seg[len(head):] + self.buf[len(self.buf) - keep:]
                break
            # tools: collect everything
            self.tool_text += self.buf
            self.buf = ""
            break
        return [(k, t) for k, t in out if t]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


class Server:
    def __init__(self, args):
        self.args = args
        t0 = time.time()
        self.eng = Engine(args.lib, args.model, args.ctx, args.draft, args.draft_vocab,
                          {"f16": 0, "q8": 1, "q4": 2, "auto": -1}[args.kv],
                          draft_model=getattr(args, "draft_model", None))
        self.template = make_template(self.eng.chat_template())
        self.model_id = os.path.basename(args.model)
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.warned = set()
        log(f"ready in {time.time() - t0:.1f} s: {self.model_id}, ctx {args.ctx}, {args.draft} drafts")
        if getattr(args, 'draft_model', None):
            log(f"DFlash verification: {os.environ.get('MK_DFLASH_VERIFY', 'full')}; "
                f"T4 IQ fusion: {os.environ.get('MK_FFN_IQ_SMALL', '0')}")

    def warn_once(self, key, msg):
        if key not in self.warned:
            self.warned.add(key)
            log("warning:", msg)

    def render(self, body):
        kw = body.get("chat_template_kwargs")
        if kw is not None and not isinstance(kw, dict):
            raise BadRequest("chat_template_kwargs must be an object")
        kw = kw or {}
        ctx = dict(messages=normalize_messages(body.get("messages") or []), add_generation_prompt=True)
        if body.get("tools"):
            ctx["tools"] = body["tools"]
        for k in ("enable_thinking", "preserve_thinking", "reasoning_effort"):
            value = kw.get(k)
            if value is None:
                value = body.get(k)
            if value is not None:
                ctx[k] = value
        for k in ("enable_thinking", "preserve_thinking"):
            if k in ctx and not isinstance(ctx[k], bool):
                raise BadRequest(f"{k} must be a boolean")
        if "reasoning_effort" in ctx:
            effort = ctx["reasoning_effort"]
            if not isinstance(effort, str) or effort not in ("none", "low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"):
                raise BadRequest("reasoning_effort must be one of none, low, medium, high, xhigh")
            if effort == "none":
                ctx["enable_thinking"] = False
                del ctx["reasoning_effort"]
            else:
                ctx["reasoning_effort"] = "xhigh" if effort == "high" else effort
        try:
            text = self.template.render(**ctx)
        except jinja2.exceptions.TemplateError as e:
            raise BadRequest(f"chat template: {e}")
        thinking = text.endswith("<think>\n")
        log("reasoning:", f"on, effort={ctx.get('reasoning_effort', 'template default')}" if thinking else "off")
        return text, thinking

    def load_prompt(self, toks):
        """brings the engine to `toks` processed, reusing what it has; returns (first token, reused count)"""
        eng = self.eng
        seq = eng.sequence()
        lcp = 0
        for a, b in zip(seq, toks):
            if a != b:
                break
            lcp += 1
        lcp = min(lcp, len(toks) - 1)  # at least one token is fed (it produces the next one)
        if seq and lcp >= eng.committed():
            eng.truncate(lcp)
            return eng.prefill(toks[lcp:]), lcp
        ck = eng.checkpoint_pos()
        if 0 < ck <= lcp and eng.rewind_to_checkpoint():
            return eng.prefill(toks[ck:]), ck
        eng.reset()
        return eng.prefill(toks), 0

    def sampling(self, body):
        temp = float(body.get("temperature", 1.0) if body.get("temperature") is not None else 1.0)
        top_p = float(body.get("top_p", 0.95) if body.get("top_p") is not None else 0.95)
        top_k = int(body.get("top_k", 20) if body.get("top_k") is not None else 20)
        min_p = float(body.get("min_p", 0.0) if body.get("min_p") is not None else 0.0)
        if not (math.isfinite(temp) and temp >= 0 and math.isfinite(top_p) and 0 < top_p <= 1
                and math.isfinite(min_p) and 0 <= min_p <= 1):
            raise BadRequest("invalid temperature, top_p or min_p")
        seed = body.get("seed")
        seed = random.getrandbits(63) if seed is None or int(seed) < 0 else int(seed)
        if top_k <= 0 or top_k > TOPK_MAX:
            if temp > 0:
                raise BadRequest(f"top_k must be in [1, {TOPK_MAX}] for non-greedy sampling")
        for k in ("presence_penalty", "frequency_penalty"):
            if float(body.get(k) or 0) != 0:
                raise BadRequest(f"{k} is not supported by the megakernel; "
                                 f"set {k}=0 in the client's Sampling settings. "
                                 "Use the llama.cpp backend to keep nonzero penalties.")
        if float(body.get("repetition_penalty") or body.get("repeat_penalty") or 1.0) != 1.0:
            raise BadRequest("repetition_penalty is not supported by the megakernel; "
                             "set repetition_penalty=1 (or repeat_penalty=1) in the client's Sampling settings. "
                             "Use the llama.cpp backend to keep repetition penalties.")
        if temp <= 0:
            self.eng.set_sampling(0.0, 1.0, TOPK_MAX, 0.0, seed)
        else:
            self.eng.set_sampling(temp, top_p, top_k, min_p, seed)

    def generate(self, body, emit):
        """runs one chat completion; emit(kind, payload) is called with ('reasoning'|'content', text) deltas.
        Returns (finish_reason, tool_calls, usage). emit returning False aborts."""
        text, thinking = self.render(body)
        toks = self.eng.tokenize(text)
        max_new = body.get("max_tokens") or body.get("max_completion_tokens") or body.get("n_predict") or -1
        max_new = int(max_new)
        limit = self.args.ctx - len(toks) - 16
        if limit <= 0:
            raise BadRequest(f"prompt of {len(toks)} tokens does not fit the context of {self.args.ctx}")
        max_new = limit if max_new <= 0 else min(max_new, limit)
        tools = body.get("tools") or []
        self.sampling(body)
        t0 = time.time()
        tok, reused = self.load_prompt(toks)
        t1 = time.time()
        parser = OutputParser(thinking, tools)
        dec = codecs.getincrementaldecoder("utf-8")(errors="replace")
        n_gen, finish, pending, aborted = 0, "length", [tok], False
        while n_gen < max_new and not aborted:
            if not pending:
                pending = self.eng.step(max_new-n_gen if self.args.draft_model else None)
            t = pending.pop(0)
            n_gen += 1
            if self.eng.is_eog(t):
                finish = "stop"
                break
            for kind, delta in parser.feed(dec.decode(self.eng.piece(t))):
                if emit(kind, delta) is False:
                    aborted = True
                    break
        for kind, delta in parser.feed(dec.decode(b"", final=True), final=True):
            emit(kind, delta)
        calls = parse_tool_calls(parser.tool_text, tools) if parser.tool_text else []
        if calls:
            finish = "tool_calls"
        t2 = time.time()
        log(f"prompt {len(toks)} tok ({reused} reused) in {t1 - t0:.2f} s, {n_gen} tok in {t2 - t1:.2f} s "
            f"({n_gen / max(t2 - t1, 1e-9):.1f} tok/s), {finish}")
        usage = {"prompt_tokens": len(toks), "completion_tokens": n_gen, "total_tokens": len(toks) + n_gen,
                 "prompt_tokens_details": {"cached_tokens": reused}}
        timings = {"prompt_n": len(toks) - reused, "prompt_ms": (t1 - t0) * 1e3, "predicted_n": n_gen,
                   "predicted_ms": (t2 - t1) * 1e3, "predicted_per_second": n_gen / max(t2 - t1, 1e-9)}
        return finish, calls, usage, timings


def make_handler(srv):
    class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"

        def log_message(self, *a):
            pass

        def _json(self, code, obj):
            data = json.dumps(obj).encode()
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(data)

        def do_GET(self):
            path = self.path.split("?")[0]
            if path in ("/health", "/v1/health"):
                self._json(200, {"status": "ok"})
            elif path in ("/v1/models", "/models"):
                self._json(200, {"object": "list", "data": [{"id": srv.model_id, "object": "model", "owned_by": "openjet"}]})
            elif path == "/props":
                self._json(200, {"default_generation_settings": {"n_ctx": srv.args.ctx}, "total_slots": 1})
            else:
                self._json(404, {"error": {"message": "not found", "type": "not_found_error"}})

        def do_POST(self):
            path = self.path.split("?")[0]
            n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
            raw = self.rfile.read(n) if n else b""
            if path not in ("/v1/chat/completions", "/chat/completions"):
                self._json(404 if not path.startswith("/slots") else 501,
                           {"error": {"message": f"{path} is not supported by the megakernel server", "type": "not_supported"}})
                return
            try:
                body = json.loads(raw or b"{}")
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                self._json(400, {"error": {"message": "invalid JSON", "type": "invalid_request_error"}})
                return
            with srv.lock:
                try:
                    if body.get("stream"):
                        self._stream(body)
                    else:
                        self._complete(body)
                except BadRequest as e:
                    self._json(400, {"error": {"message": str(e), "type": "invalid_request_error"}})
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
                except Exception as e:  # engine failure: report it, keep serving
                    log("error:", repr(e))
                    try:
                        self._json(500, {"error": {"message": str(e), "type": "server_error"}})
                    except Exception:
                        pass

        def _complete(self, body):
            parts = {"reasoning": [], "content": []}
            finish, calls, usage, timings = srv.generate(body, lambda k, t: parts[k].append(t))
            msg = {"role": "assistant", "content": "".join(parts["content"]) or (None if calls else "")}
            if parts["reasoning"]:
                msg["reasoning_content"] = "".join(parts["reasoning"]).strip()
            if calls:
                msg["tool_calls"] = calls
            self._json(200, {"id": "chatcmpl-" + uuid.uuid4().hex, "object": "chat.completion", "created": int(time.time()),
                             "model": srv.model_id, "choices": [{"index": 0, "message": msg, "finish_reason": finish}],
                             "usage": usage, "timings": timings})

        def _stream(self, body):
            cid, created = "chatcmpl-" + uuid.uuid4().hex, int(time.time())
            started = [False]

            def chunk(delta, finish=None, usage=None, timings=None):
                obj = {"id": cid, "object": "chat.completion.chunk", "created": created, "model": srv.model_id,
                       "choices": [] if delta is None else [{"index": 0, "delta": delta, "finish_reason": finish}]}
                if usage is not None:
                    obj["usage"] = usage
                if timings is not None:
                    obj["timings"] = timings
                data = b"data: " + json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False).encode() + b"\n\n"
                self.wfile.write(b"%x\r\n" % len(data) + data + b"\r\n")
                self.wfile.flush()

            def head():
                if not started[0]:
                    started[0] = True
                    self.send_response(200)
                    self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
                    self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                    self.end_headers()
                    chunk({"role": "assistant", "content": None})

            def emit(kind, text):
                head()
                try:
                    chunk({"reasoning_content": text} if kind == "reasoning" else {"content": text})
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    return False
                return True

            # render / tokenize errors are reported as a 400 before the stream starts
            srv.render(body)
            finish, calls, usage, timings = srv.generate(body, emit)
            head()
            for i, c in enumerate(calls):
                chunk({"tool_calls": [{"index": i, **c}]})
            chunk({}, finish=finish, timings=timings)
            if (body.get("stream_options") or {}).get("include_usage"):
                chunk(None, usage=usage)
            data = b"data: [DONE]\n\n"
            self.wfile.write(b"%x\r\n" % len(data) + data + b"\r\n0\r\n\r\n")
            self.wfile.flush()

    return H


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("-m", "--model", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=18080)
    ap.add_argument("-c", "--ctx", type=int, default=32768)
    ap.add_argument("-d", "--draft", type=int)
    ap.add_argument("--draft-vocab", default=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(HERE), "draft_vocab_32k.bin"))
    ap.add_argument("--draft-model", help="native DFlash2 GGUF; --draft selects 1..7 proposals")
    ap.add_argument("--verify-width", choices=("full", "3", "auto"),
                    help="native DFlash2 only; keeps the trained draft block, optionally verifies a shorter prefix")
    ap.add_argument("--prefill-chunk", default="auto", help="auto by free VRAM, or 128..2048 in multiples of 128")
    ap.add_argument("--lib", default=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(HERE), "libqwen38mk.so"))
    ap.add_argument("--kv", choices=["auto", "f16", "q8", "q4"], default="auto",
                    help="Target KV cache format; q4 is lossy symmetric int4 (auto chooses FP16/Q8 only)")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.verify_width is not None:
        if not args.draft_model:
            ap.error('--verify-width requires a native --draft-model')
        os.environ['MK_DFLASH_VERIFY'] = args.verify_width
        if args.verify_width != 'full':
            os.environ.setdefault('MK_FFN_IQ_SMALL', '1')
    if args.draft is None:
        args.draft = 7 if args.draft_model else 4 if model_mtp_layers(args.lib, args.model) else 0
    os.environ["MK_PREFILL_CHUNK"] = str(args.prefill_chunk)
    srv = Server(args)
    httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), make_handler(srv))
    httpd.daemon_threads = True
    log(f"listening on http://{args.host}:{args.port}")
    try:
        httpd.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

END FILE

FILE: megakernel/capi.cpp
// C API over MKEngine plus libllama's tokenizer (vocab only), for the HTTP server (server/mk_server.py).
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <memory>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "llama.h"
#include "qwen38_mk.h"
#include "dflash_native.h"
#include "model_spec.h"
#include "dflash_verify_policy.h"

namespace {
struct Handle {
    MKEngine * eng = nullptr;
    llama_model * lm = nullptr;
    const llama_vocab * vocab = nullptr;
    MKDFlashNative * dflash=nullptr;
    int draft_depth=0;
    std::unique_ptr<DFlashVerifyPolicy> verify_policy;
    std::string err;
    ~Handle() { delete dflash;delete eng; if (lm) llama_model_free(lm); }
};
thread_local std::string g_err;
}

extern "C" {

const char * mk_last_error() { return g_err.c_str(); }

// Host-only schema inspection, including standard Swift and stripped targets.
// No CUDA initialization, tokenizer allocation, or weight upload is needed.
int mk_model_mtp_layers(const char * model) {
    try {
        if (!model) throw std::runtime_error("missing model path");
        gguf::File file(model);
        gguf::validate_qwen38(file, false);
        const auto it = file.ints.find("qwen35.nextn_predict_layers");
        return it == file.ints.end() ? 0 : int(it->second);
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        g_err = e.what();
        return -1;
    }
}

void * mk_new(const char * model, int ctx, int n_draft, const char * draft_vocab, int kv_mode) {
    try {
        llama_backend_init();
        llama_model_params mp = llama_model_default_params();
        mp.vocab_only = true;
        auto h = std::make_unique<Handle>();
        h->lm = llama_model_load_from_file(model, mp);
        if (!h->lm) throw std::runtime_error("failed to load the vocabulary");
        h->vocab = llama_model_get_vocab(h->lm);
        h->eng = new MKEngine(model, ctx, n_draft, draft_vocab ? draft_vocab : "", kv_mode);
        return h.release();
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        g_err = e.what();
        return nullptr;
    }
}

void mk_free(void * hp) {
    auto * h = static_cast<Handle *>(hp);
    delete h;
}

void * mk_new_dflash(const char * model,const char * draft,int ctx,int depth,int kv_mode) {
    try {
        if(!model||!draft||depth<1||depth>7)throw std::runtime_error("DFlash2 needs target, draft and depth in [1,7]");
        llama_backend_init();auto mp=llama_model_default_params();mp.vocab_only=true;
        auto h=std::make_unique<Handle>();
        h->lm=llama_model_load_from_file(model,mp);
        if(!h->lm)throw std::runtime_error("cannot load target vocabulary");
        h->vocab=llama_model_get_vocab(h->lm);
        std::unique_ptr<llama_model,decltype(&llama_model_free)> dm(llama_model_load_from_file(draft,mp),llama_model_free);
        if(!dm)throw std::runtime_error("cannot load draft vocabulary");
        const auto * dv=llama_model_get_vocab(dm.get());
        if(llama_vocab_n_tokens(dv)!=llama_vocab_n_tokens(h->vocab)||llama_vocab_bos(dv)!=llama_vocab_bos(h->vocab)||
           llama_vocab_eos(dv)!=llama_vocab_eos(h->vocab)||llama_vocab_type(dv)!=llama_vocab_type(h->vocab))
            throw std::runtime_error("draft/target vocabulary metadata differ");
        for(int id=0;id<llama_vocab_n_tokens(dv);id++)
            if(std::strcmp(llama_vocab_get_text(dv,id),llama_vocab_get_text(h->vocab,id))||
               llama_vocab_get_attr(dv,id)!=llama_vocab_get_attr(h->vocab,id))
                throw std::runtime_error("draft/target token tables differ");
        // Draft weights, sliding KV/checkpoint, encoder workspace, feature
        // capture and CUDA graph/library margin must precede auto chunk sizing.
        h->eng=new MKEngine(model,ctx,0,"",kv_mode,size_t(1792)*1024*1024);
        h->dflash=new MKDFlashNative(*h->eng,draft,ctx,llama_vocab_mask(dv));h->draft_depth=depth;
        h->verify_policy=std::make_unique<DFlashVerifyPolicy>(depth,DFlashVerifyPolicy::environment());
        auto * owner=h.get();
        h->eng->enable_device_features(h->dflash->layers(),[owner](int pos,int n,const float * f){owner->dflash->inject_device(pos,n,f);});
        return h.release();
    } catch(const std::exception & e){g_err=e.what();return nullptr;}
}

// chat template stored in the GGUF (tokenizer.chat_template); returns its length, copies up to cap
int mk_chat_template(void * hp, char * buf, int cap) {
    auto * h = static_cast<Handle *>(hp);
    const char * t = llama_model_chat_template(h->lm, nullptr);
    if (!t) return -1;
    const int n = (int) strlen(t);
    if (buf && cap > 0) memcpy(buf, t, std::min(n, cap));
    return n;
}

// text -> token ids (special tokens such as [special:im_start] parsed); returns the count (negative: -needed)
int mk_tokenize(void * hp, const char * text, int len, int * out, int cap) {
    auto * h = static_cast<Handle *>(hp);
    return llama_tokenize(h->vocab, text, len, out, cap, false, true);
}

// token -> its bytes (special tokens rendered as text); returns the byte count
int mk_piece(void * hp, int tok, char * buf, int cap) {
    auto * h = static_cast<Handle *>(hp);
    return llama_token_to_piece(h->vocab, tok, buf, cap, 0, true);
}

int mk_is_eog(void * hp, int tok) { return llama_vocab_is_eog(static_cast<Handle *>(hp)->vocab, tok) ? 1 : 0; }

int mk_set_sampling(void * hp, float temp, float top_p, int top_k, float min_p, unsigned long long seed) {
    try {
        static_cast<Handle *>(hp)->eng->set_sampling(temp, top_p, top_k, min_p, seed);
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        g_err = e.what();
        return -1;
    }
}

// appends tokens to the sequence; returns the first generated token (negative on error)
int mk_prefill(void * hp, const int * toks, int n) {
    try {
        auto * h=static_cast<Handle *>(hp);
        if(!toks||n<1)throw std::runtime_error("prefill requires token IDs");
        int first=h->eng->prefill(std::vector<int>(toks,toks+n));
        if(h->dflash){h->dflash->checkpoint();h->verify_policy->reset();}
        return first;
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        g_err = e.what();
        return -1;
    }
}

// One decode cycle. DFlash bounds verification/output by cap, preserving its
// full trained draft block; cap=1 commits the anchor without a speculative draft.
// MTP retains its minimum buffer capacity. Returns the count (negative on error).
int mk_step(void * hp, int * out, int cap) {
    try {
        std::vector<int> v;
        auto * h=static_cast<Handle *>(hp);
        if(!out||cap<1||(!h->dflash&&cap<h->eng->n_draft()+1))throw std::runtime_error("step output capacity is too small");
        if(h->dflash) {
            std::vector<int> proposals;
            const int width=h->verify_policy->width();
            if(cap>1) {
                proposals=h->dflash->draft(h->eng->position(),h->eng->sequence().back(),h->draft_depth);
                proposals.resize(std::min(width,cap-1));
            }
            const long before=h->eng->stats.accepted;
            h->eng->verify_external(proposals,v);
            if(int(proposals.size())==width)
                h->verify_policy->observe(width,int(h->eng->stats.accepted-before));
        } else h->eng->step(v);
        const int n = std::min((int) v.size(), cap);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) out[i] = v[i];
        return n;
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        g_err = e.what();
        return -1;
    }
}

void mk_reset(void * hp) { auto * h=static_cast<Handle *>(hp);h->eng->reset();if(h->dflash){h->dflash->reset();h->verify_policy->reset();} }

int mk_truncate(void * hp, int len) {
    try {
        auto * h=static_cast<Handle *>(hp);h->eng->truncate(len);
        if(h->dflash){h->dflash->truncate(len);h->verify_policy->reset();}
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        g_err = e.what();
        return -1;
    }
}

// copies the sequence so far (up to cap tokens); returns its length
int mk_sequence(void * hp, int * out, int cap) {
    const auto & s = static_cast<Handle *>(hp)->eng->sequence();
    const int n = (int) s.size();
    if (out)
        for (int i = 0; i < std::min(n, cap); i++) out[i] = s[i];
    return n;
}

int mk_committed(void * hp) { return static_cast<Handle *>(hp)->eng->committed(); }
int mk_checkpoint_pos(void * hp) { return static_cast<Handle *>(hp)->eng->checkpoint_pos(); }
int mk_rewind_to_checkpoint(void * hp) {
    try {auto * h=static_cast<Handle *>(hp);
        if(!h->eng->rewind_to_checkpoint())return -1;
        if(h->dflash&&!h->dflash->restore_checkpoint())throw std::runtime_error("draft checkpoint missing");
        if(h->verify_policy)h->verify_policy->reset();
        return 0;
    } catch(const std::exception & e){g_err=e.what();return -1;}
}

void mk_stats(void * hp, long * cycles, long * drafted, long * accepted) {
    const auto & s = static_cast<Handle *>(hp)->eng->stats;
    *cycles = s.cycles;
    *drafted = s.drafted;
    *accepted = s.accepted;
}

} // extern "C"

END FILE

FILE: megakernel/qwen38_mk.cu
// Persistent cooperative "megakernel" for Qwen3.8-27B (llama.cpp arch "qwen35", shared with Qwen3.5/3.6;
// 64 layers: 48 Gated-DeltaNet + 16 gated full-attention, plus one MTP block), tuned for
// RTX 3090 (sm_86, 82 SMs).
//
// One kernel launch executes an entire speculative decoding cycle:
//   main forward over T = 1 + n_draft tokens  ->  lm_head + greedy verify on device
//   -> MTP catch-up rows  ->  n_draft MTP draft steps.
// Phases inside the launch are separated by a software grid barrier.  Weights stay in their
// GGUF k-quant encodings (Q6_K / Q8_0 are re-laid-out for aligned loads, values unchanged)
// and are streamed once per cycle with int8 dp4a dot products against q8 activations.
//
// Gated-DeltaNet rollback after rejected drafts is done by lazy replay: the committed state
// only ever includes surely-accepted tokens; accepted drafts are replayed from a small ring
// of (k, v, g, beta) at the start of the next cycle, so no state snapshots are written.

#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cooperative_groups.h>
#include <cublas_v2.h>
#include <mma.h>
#include "ggml.h"
#include "ggml-alloc.h"
#include "ggml-backend.h"
#include "ggml-cuda.h"
#include "native_quant.cuh"
#include "kv_quant.cuh"
#include "native_iq_mma.cuh"

#include <algorithm>
#include <map>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

#include "gguf.h"
#include "model_spec.h"
#include "qwen38_mk.h"

#define CK(x)                                                                                   \
    do {                                                                                        \
        cudaError_t e_ = (x);                                                                   \
        if (e_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                \
            fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\n", #x, __FILE__, __LINE__,             \
                    cudaGetErrorString(e_));                                                    \
            throw std::runtime_error(std::string("CUDA: ") + cudaGetErrorString(e_));                                                                            \
        }                                                                                       \
    } while (0)

namespace {

constexpr int D = 5120, FF = 17408, NL = 64, NLA = 65;
constexpr int NH = 24, NKV = 4, HD = 256;
constexpr int NKH = 16, NVH = 48, HK = 128;
constexpr int QKV = 10240, VD = 6144, QG = 12288, KVD = 1024;
constexpr int MAXT = 8;
constexpr int NT = 512, NW = NT / 32;
constexpr float EPS = 1e-6f;
constexpr float ROPE_BASE = 10000000.0f;
constexpr int CONV_RING = 16, KV_RING = 16, H_RING = 16;
static_assert(CONV_RING>=MAXT+3,"new tokens must not overwrite convolution history");
constexpr int KVGB = NKH * HK + NVH * HK + 2 * NVH; // k | v | g | beta
constexpr int KVGB_V = NKH * HK, KVGB_G = KVGB_V + NVH * HK, KVGB_B = KVGB_G + NVH;
constexpr int N_ATT_SLOTS = 17; // 16 trunk attention layers + MTP block
constexpr int MAX_CH = 32;      // attention split-K chunks per kv head
constexpr int DV_EXTRA = 4096;  // draft-vocabulary rows reserved for tokens of the current sequence
constexpr int AB_SPLIT = 4;     // K-chunks per delta-net alpha/beta row
constexpr int U_D = D / 32, U_FF = FF / 32, U_VD = VD / 32, U_EH = 2 * D / 32;
constexpr int UMAX = U_FF;

constexpr int AR = 6 * MAXT;   // attention query rows per kv head (6 q heads x tokens)
constexpr int SM_XU = 4 * UMAX; // activation units (x tokens) shared memory holds; T = 5 ffn_down fills in two halves
constexpr int SM_XA = 0;
constexpr int SM_XB = SM_XA + SM_XU * 16;
constexpr int SM_XM = SM_XB + SM_XU * 16;
constexpr int SM_MISC = SM_XM + SM_XU * 8;
constexpr int ATP = 32; // attention: positions per K/V tile (two tiles in flight)
constexpr int KVQ_ROW = 256 + 16; // staged q8 K or V row: 256 int8 values + 8 fp16 scales
// K/V tile region: fp16 cache: 2 stages of fp16 K/V tiles; q8 cache: one fp16 K/V tile + 2 stages of q8 tiles
constexpr int SM_ATTN_KV = (4 * ATP * (256 + 8) * 2 > 2 * ATP * (256 + 8) * 2 + 4 * ATP * KVQ_ROW)
                               ? 4 * ATP * (256 + 8) * 2 : 2 * ATP * (256 + 8) * 2 + 4 * ATP * KVQ_ROW;
constexpr int SM_ATTN = 32 * (256 + 8) * 2 + SM_ATTN_KV + 32 * ATP * 4 + 32 * (ATP + 8) * 2 + 96 * 4; // Q, K/V, S, P, stats
constexpr int SM_TOTAL = (SM_MISC + 2048 > SM_ATTN ? SM_MISC + 2048 : SM_ATTN);
static_assert(SM_TOTAL <= 99 * 1024, "shared memory budget");

enum { T_F32 = 0, T_Q8_0 = 8, T_Q4K = 12, T_Q5K = 13, T_Q6K = 14, T_ANY = -1 };
enum { MODE_PREFILL = 0, MODE_DECODE = 1, MODE_BENCH = 2 };

struct Mat {
    const uint8_t * w;
    const __half * d; // per-row block scales for repacked Q6_K / Q8_0
    int type, K, N, rb, nd;
};

struct LayerW {
    int attn;
    const float *attn_norm, *post_norm;
    // gated delta net
    Mat qkv, gate, sout;
    const float *ab, *conv, *dt, *A, *snorm;
    // gated attention
    Mat q, k, v, o;
    const float *qn, *kn;
    // ffn
    Mat fg, fu, fd;
};

struct ModelW {
    LayerW L[NLA];
    Mat eh, head;
    Mat head_d;            // MTP draft head: subset of lm_head rows (== head when no subset is used)
    const int * draft_ids; // row -> token id for head_d (nullptr: identity)
    const float *enorm, *hnorm, *shnorm, *onorm;
    Mat embedding;
    const uint8_t * embd;
};

// Quantised activation vector(s) in the "unit" layout consumed by the GEMV:
// unit u = (super-block b, 64-group p, half h) covers elements A=[256b+64p+16h,+16) and
// B=[256b+64p+32+16h,+16).  xa/xb hold the int8 values, d the per-32 scales of the natural
// blocks the A/B halves live in, qs the int sums of each 16-chunk, ssq per natural block x^2.
struct QBuf {
    int8_t *xa, *xb;
    uint2 * meta; // per unit: half2 (scale of A / B chunk), short2 (int sums of A / B chunk)
    float * ssq;
    int U;
};

struct Ctl {
    int n;         // position of tok[0]
    int tok[8];    // tokens fed to the main model this cycle
    int dn_c;      // delta-net committed position (state covers positions < dn_c)
    int mtp_valid; // MTP kv valid for positions < mtp_valid
    int n_out, out[8];
    int n_acc;
    int mtp_R, mtp_logits, mtp_P;
    int mtp_pos[8], mtp_tok[8];
    int tgt[8];
    int pad[8];
};

struct Bufs {
    float *x, *xm;
    QBuf qn, qo, qa, qe;
    float *z, *ab, *qbuf, *kbuf, *vbuf;
    float *conv, *kvgb, *dstate;
    void *kc, *vc;     // KV payload [slot][kv head][ctx]: FP16, signed Q8 or packed signed Q4
    __half *ksc, *vsc; // ... with one fp16 scale per 32 values: [slot][kv head][ctx][HD / 32]
    int kv_mode;
    float * apart;
    float2 * aml;
    float *h_hist, *hm;
    const float2 * rope; // [ctx][32] (cos, sin) of pos * base^(-2i/64)
    float2 * blk_best; // per block: best (logit, id) per row, or the TOPK_MAX best when sampling
    float2 * cand;     // [MAXT][TOPK_MAX] best (logit, id) of the main lm_head rows, descending (sampling)
    unsigned * bar;
    unsigned * cnt;
    Ctl * ctl;
    unsigned long long * prof; // optional per-barrier timestamps (block 0)
    int ctx;
    int * numeric_error; // optional finite-value diagnostics
    float * features;
    int feature_map[NL];
    int n_features;
    bool ffn_iq_fused;
    bool iq_inline;
    bool ffn_iq_mma;
    bool ffn_down_global;
    bool iq_paired;
    bool ffn_iq_small; // Ada T=4 experiment: same ordered IQ dots, shared gate/up input loads
#ifdef MK_PHASE_ONLY
    float * gu_probe; // isolated-test diagnostics; absent from production buffers
#endif
};

__constant__ ModelW cW;
__constant__ Bufs cB;

struct Launch {
    int mode, T, last, ndraft;
    int score; // prefill only: greedy argmax for every row (teacher-forced evaluation), no MTP
    // sampling (temp > 0; greedy otherwise), applied in llama.cpp's order: top_k, top_p, min_p, temperature
    float temp, top_p, min_p;
    int top_k; // <= TOPK_MAX
    unsigned long long seed;
};
constexpr int TOPK_MAX = 32; // candidates kept per main lm_head row when sampling

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// small device helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Weights are streamed once per cycle: bypass L1 and mark them evict-first in L2 so that data
// prefetched with evict-last (recurrent state, KV cache) survives the stream.
__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned long long l2_evict_first() {
    unsigned long long pol;
    asm("createpolicy.fractional.L2::evict_first.b64 %0, 1.0;" : "=l"(pol));
    return pol;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ int4 ldw4(const void * p) {
    int4 r;
    asm("ld.global.nc.L1::no_allocate.L2::cache_hint.v4.s32 {%0,%1,%2,%3}, [%4], %5;"
        : "=r"(r.x), "=r"(r.y), "=r"(r.z), "=r"(r.w)
        : "l"(p), "l"(l2_evict_first()));
    return r;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ int2 ldw2(const void * p) {
    int2 r;
    asm("ld.global.nc.L1::no_allocate.L2::cache_hint.v2.s32 {%0,%1}, [%2], %3;" : "=r"(r.x), "=r"(r.y) : "l"(p), "l"(l2_evict_first()));
    return r;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ int ldw1(const void * p) {
    int r;
    asm("ld.global.nc.L1::no_allocate.L2::cache_hint.s32 %0, [%1], %2;" : "=r"(r) : "l"(p), "l"(l2_evict_first()));
    return r;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void pf_l2(const void * p) { asm volatile("prefetch.global.L2::evict_last [%0];" ::"l"(p)); }
// prefetch `bytes` starting at p (128-byte lines) with the threads of the calling block
__device__ __forceinline__ void pf_block(const void * p, int bytes) {
    for (int off = threadIdx.x * 128; off < bytes; off += NT * 128) pf_l2(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(p) + off);
}
__device__ __forceinline__ float4 ldw4f(const float * p) {
    int4 r = ldw4(p);
    return make_float4(__int_as_float(r.x), __int_as_float(r.y), __int_as_float(r.z), __int_as_float(r.w));
}
__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned short ldwu16(const void * p) {
    unsigned short r;
    asm("ld.global.nc.L1::no_allocate.u16 %0, [%1];" : "=h"(r) : "l"(p));
    return r;
}

__device__ __forceinline__ float wsum(float v) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) v += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, o);
    return v;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ float wmax(float v) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) v = fmaxf(v, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, o));
    return v;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ float silu(float x) { return x / (1.0f + expf(-x)); }

enum {
    PH_EMBED, PH_ATTN_PROJ, PH_ATTN_CORE, PH_ATTN_COMB, PH_RESID_O, PH_DN_PROJ, PH_DN_CORE, PH_RESID_SOUT, PH_FFN_GU,
    PH_FFN_DOWN, PH_LMHEAD, PH_MTP_M0, PH_MTP_EH, PH_MTP_ATTN_PROJ, PH_MTP_ATTN_CORE, PH_MTP_ATTN_COMB, PH_MTP_RESID_O,
    PH_MTP_FFN_GU, PH_MTP_FFN_DOWN, PH_MTP_LMHEAD, PH_COUNT
};

__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned long long gtimer() {
    unsigned long long t;
    asm volatile("mov.u64 %0, %%globaltimer;" : "=l"(t));
    return t;
}

__shared__ unsigned s_bar_k; // index of the last grid barrier passed (profiling only)
extern __shared__ __align__(16) uint8_t smem[]; // dynamic shared memory of the persistent block

// CUDA cooperative grid barrier publishes all phase writes before the next phase.
__device__ __forceinline__ void gsync(unsigned & epoch, int tag) {
    __syncthreads();
    if (threadIdx.x == 0 && cB.prof) {
        const unsigned k=epoch/gridDim.x+1;
        if(k<1024) cB.prof[4096+k*128+blockIdx.x]=gtimer();
    }
    cooperative_groups::this_grid().sync();
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
        epoch += gridDim.x;
        if(cB.prof && blockIdx.x==0) {
            const unsigned k=epoch/gridDim.x;
            s_bar_k=k;
            if(k<2048) cB.prof[k]=(gtimer()<<8)|unsigned(tag);
        }
    }
    __syncthreads();
    if (cB.numeric_error && blockIdx.x == 0) {
        const float * values = nullptr;
        int count=0;
        if (tag==PH_EMBED || tag==PH_RESID_O || tag==PH_RESID_SOUT || tag==PH_FFN_DOWN) {
            values=cB.x;count=MAXT*D;
        } else if(tag==PH_ATTN_PROJ || tag==PH_MTP_ATTN_PROJ) {
            values=cB.qbuf;count=MAXT*QG;
        } else if(tag==PH_DN_PROJ) { values=cB.z;count=MAXT*VD; }
        else if(tag==PH_MTP_EH || tag==PH_MTP_RESID_O || tag==PH_MTP_FFN_DOWN) { values=cB.xm;count=MAXT*D; }
        for(int i=threadIdx.x;values && i<count;i+=NT) {
            if(!isfinite(__ldcg(values+i)) && atomicCAS(cB.numeric_error,0,1)==0)
                printf("[mk-numeric] nonfinite n=%d phase=%d barrier=%u index=%d\n",cB.ctl->n,tag,epoch/gridDim.x,i);
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
}

// profiling: timestamp the end of a phase's activation fill (block 0)
__device__ __forceinline__ void mark_fill() {
    if (cB.prof && blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0 && s_bar_k + 1 < 2048) cB.prof[2048 + s_bar_k + 1] = gtimer();
}

// block-wide sum, result broadcast. red must hold >= NW floats.
__device__ __forceinline__ float bsum(float v, float * red) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    v = wsum(v);
    __syncthreads();
    if (lane == 0) red[warp] = v;
    __syncthreads();
    float r = lane < NW ? red[lane] : 0.f;
    r = wsum(r);
    return r;
}

// lane i holds element 32*j+i of row t; quantise the natural 32-block j into the unit layout.
__device__ __forceinline__ void q32_store(const QBuf & q, int t, int j, float v, float ss, bool wss) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    const float amax = wmax(fabsf(v));
    const float d = amax / 127.0f;
    const int qi = amax == 0.0f ? 0 : (int) roundf(v / d);
    const int b = j >> 3, p = (j >> 1) & 3, part = j & 1, hh = lane >> 4, byte = lane & 15;
    const int gi = t * q.U + 8 * b + 2 * p + hh;
    (part ? q.xb : q.xa)[gi * 16 + byte] = (int8_t) qi;
    int qs = qi;
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 8);
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 4);
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 2);
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 1);
    if (byte == 0) {
        reinterpret_cast<__half *>(q.meta + gi)[part] = __float2half_rn(d);
        reinterpret_cast<short *>(q.meta + gi)[2 + part] = (short) qs;
    }
    if (wss) {
        const float s = wsum(ss);
        if (lane == 0) q.ssq[t * q.U + j] = s;
    }
}

struct SX {
    int4 * xa;
    int4 * xb;
    uint2 * xm; // per unit: half2 scales of the A / B 16-chunks, short2 int sums of the chunks
    int U;
};

__device__ __forceinline__ SX make_sx(uint8_t * /*sm*/, int U) {
    uint8_t * const sm = smem;
    SX s;
    s.xa = reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(sm + SM_XA);
    s.xb = reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(sm + SM_XB);
    s.xm = reinterpret_cast<uint2 *>(sm + SM_XM);
    s.U = U;
    return s;
}

__device__ __forceinline__ float * misc(uint8_t * /*sm*/) {
    uint8_t * const sm = smem; return reinterpret_cast<float *>(sm + SM_MISC); }

__device__ __forceinline__ void cp_async16(void * smem_dst, const void * gsrc) {
    const unsigned d = (unsigned) __cvta_generic_to_shared(smem_dst);
    asm volatile("cp.async.cg.shared.global [%0], [%1], 16;" ::"r"(d), "l"(gsrc) : "memory");
}

// Load T rows (starting at row t0) of a quantised activation buffer into shared memory with
// async copies.  norm: s_rs[t] receives the RMS-norm scale of row t (from the producer's
// per-block sums of squares); consumers apply it to their dot products, otherwise s_rs = 1.
template <int T>
__device__ void fill_xs(const QBuf & q, int t0, bool norm, const SX & s, float * s_rs) {
    const int tid = threadIdx.x, lane = tid & 31, warp = tid >> 5;
    const int U = q.U;
    const int8_t * ga = q.xa + (size_t) t0 * U * 16;
    const int8_t * gb = q.xb + (size_t) t0 * U * 16;
    const uint2 * gm = q.meta + (size_t) t0 * U;
    for (int i = tid; i < T * U; i += NT) {
        cp_async16(s.xa + i, ga + (size_t) i * 16);
        cp_async16(s.xb + i, gb + (size_t) i * 16);
    }
    for (int i = tid; i < T * U / 2; i += NT) cp_async16(s.xm + 2 * i, gm + 2 * i);
    asm volatile("cp.async.commit_group;" ::: "memory");
    if (norm) { // normed consumers always read the K = D residual stream (U_D = 160 partial sums)
        if (warp < T) {
            float p[U_D / 32];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < U_D / 32; k++) p[k] = __ldcg(q.ssq + (t0 + warp) * U_D + lane + 32 * k);
            float acc = 0.f;
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < U_D / 32; k++) acc += p[k];
            acc = wsum(acc);
            if (lane == 0) s_rs[warp] = rsqrtf(acc / (float) D + EPS);
        }
    } else if (tid < T) {
        s_rs[tid] = 1.0f;
    }
    asm volatile("cp.async.wait_all;" ::: "memory");
    __syncthreads();
    mark_fill();
}

// Async copy of units [u0, u0 + US) of T activation rows (one K-slice) into shared memory.
template <int T, int US>
__device__ void fill_xs_part(const QBuf & q, int u0, const SX & s) {
    const int tid = threadIdx.x, U = q.U;
    __syncthreads(); // the previous slice may still be in use
    for (int i = tid; i < T * US; i += NT) {
        const int t = i / US, u = i - t * US;
        const size_t gi = (size_t) t * U + u0 + u;
        cp_async16(s.xa + i, q.xa + gi * 16);
        cp_async16(s.xb + i, q.xb + gi * 16);
    }
    for (int i = tid; i < T * US / 2; i += NT) {
        const int t = 2 * i / US, u = 2 * i - t * US;
        cp_async16(s.xm + 2 * i, q.meta + (size_t) t * U + u0 + u);
    }
    asm volatile("cp.async.commit_group;\ncp.async.wait_all;" ::: "memory");
    __syncthreads();
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// quantised GEMV: R rows per warp share every activation load (per-lane partial sums;
// the caller reduces across the warp).  Lane handles units u = lane + 32 i.
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

constexpr int M4 = 0x0F0F0F0F;

__device__ __forceinline__ int gwarp() { return (threadIdx.x >> 5) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x; }
__device__ __forceinline__ int nwarps() { return NW * gridDim.x; }

template <int T, int R>
__device__ __forceinline__ void zero_acc(float (&a)[R][T]) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < R; k++)
#pragma unroll
        for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) a[k][t] = 0.f;
}

template <int T, int R>
__device__ __forceinline__ void wsum_acc(float (&a)[R][T]) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < R; k++)
#pragma unroll
        for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) a[k][t] = wsum(a[k][t]);
}

template <int T, int R, int UU>
__device__ __forceinline__ void accR(const SX & s, int u, const int (&A)[R][4], const int (&B)[R][4],
                                     const float (&fA)[R], const float (&gA)[R], const float (&fB)[R],
                                     const float (&gB)[R], float (&acc)[R][T]) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) {
        const int ix = t * UU + u;
        const int4 va = s.xa[ix], vb = s.xb[ix];
        const uint2 mm = s.xm[ix];
        const float2 dx = __half22float2(*reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(&mm.x));
        const int qq = (int) mm.y;
        const float sqA = dx.x * (float) (short) (qq & 0xffff), sqB = dx.y * (float) (short) (qq >> 16);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            int sa = __dp4a(A[k][0], va.x, 0);
            sa = __dp4a(A[k][1], va.y, sa);
            sa = __dp4a(A[k][2], va.z, sa);
            sa = __dp4a(A[k][3], va.w, sa);
            int sb = __dp4a(B[k][0], vb.x, 0);
            sb = __dp4a(B[k][1], vb.y, sb);
            sb = __dp4a(B[k][2], vb.z, sb);
            sb = __dp4a(B[k][3], vb.w, sb);
            acc[k][t] += fA[k] * (dx.x * (float) sa) + fB[k] * (dx.y * (float) sb) - gA[k] * sqA - gB[k] * sqB;
        }
    }
}

__device__ __forceinline__ void q4k_scales(const int4 & h, int p, float & fA, float & gA, float & fB, float & gB) {
    const float2 dm = __half22float2(*reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(&h.x));
    const int sh = (p & 1) * 16;
    const unsigned y = (unsigned) h.y >> sh, z = (unsigned) h.z >> sh, w = (unsigned) h.w >> sh;
    unsigned sc0, sc1, m0, m1;
    if (p < 2) {
        sc0 = y & 63;
        sc1 = (y >> 8) & 63;
        m0 = z & 63;
        m1 = (z >> 8) & 63;
    } else {
        sc0 = (w & 0xF) | ((y >> 2) & 0x30);
        sc1 = ((w >> 8) & 0xF) | ((y >> 10) & 0x30);
        m0 = ((w >> 4) & 0xF) | ((z >> 2) & 0x30);
        m1 = ((w >> 12) & 0xF) | ((z >> 10) & 0x30);
    }
    fA = dm.x * (float) sc0;
    fB = dm.x * (float) sc1;
    gA = dm.y * (float) m0;
    gB = dm.y * (float) m1;
}

template <int T, int IT, int R>
__device__ __forceinline__ void rows_q4k(const uint8_t * const (&rp)[R], const SX & s, float (&acc)[R][T]) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
#pragma unroll(IT > 8 ? (T >= 5 ? 1 : 4) : IT)
    for (int i = 0; i < IT; i++) {
        const int u = lane + 32 * i, b = u >> 3, p = (u >> 1) & 3, hh = u & 1;
        int4 h[R], q[R];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            const uint8_t * blk = rp[k] + b * 144;
            h[k] = ldw4(blk);
            q[k] = ldw4(blk + 16 + 32 * p + 16 * hh);
        }
        int A[R][4], B[R][4];
        float fA[R], gA[R], fB[R], gB[R];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            q4k_scales(h[k], p, fA[k], gA[k], fB[k], gB[k]);
            A[k][0] = q[k].x & M4; A[k][1] = q[k].y & M4; A[k][2] = q[k].z & M4; A[k][3] = q[k].w & M4;
            B[k][0] = (q[k].x >> 4) & M4; B[k][1] = (q[k].y >> 4) & M4;
            B[k][2] = (q[k].z >> 4) & M4; B[k][3] = (q[k].w >> 4) & M4;
        }
        accR<T, R, 32 * IT>(s, u, A, B, fA, gA, fB, gB, acc);
    }
}

template <int T, int IT, int R>
__device__ __forceinline__ void rows_q5k(const uint8_t * const (&rp)[R], const SX & s, float (&acc)[R][T]) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
#pragma unroll(IT > 8 ? (T >= 5 ? 1 : 4) : IT)
    for (int i = 0; i < IT; i++) {
        const int u = lane + 32 * i, b = u >> 3, p = (u >> 1) & 3, hh = u & 1;
        int4 h[R], qh[R], q[R];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            const uint8_t * blk = rp[k] + b * 176;
            h[k] = ldw4(blk);
            qh[k] = ldw4(blk + 16 + 16 * hh);
            q[k] = ldw4(blk + 48 + 32 * p + 16 * hh);
        }
        int A[R][4], B[R][4];
        float fA[R], gA[R], fB[R], gB[R];
        const int s0 = 2 * p, s1 = 2 * p + 1;
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            q4k_scales(h[k], p, fA[k], gA[k], fB[k], gB[k]);
            A[k][0] = (q[k].x & M4) | (((qh[k].x >> s0) & 0x01010101) << 4);
            A[k][1] = (q[k].y & M4) | (((qh[k].y >> s0) & 0x01010101) << 4);
            A[k][2] = (q[k].z & M4) | (((qh[k].z >> s0) & 0x01010101) << 4);
            A[k][3] = (q[k].w & M4) | (((qh[k].w >> s0) & 0x01010101) << 4);
            B[k][0] = ((q[k].x >> 4) & M4) | (((qh[k].x >> s1) & 0x01010101) << 4);
            B[k][1] = ((q[k].y >> 4) & M4) | (((qh[k].y >> s1) & 0x01010101) << 4);
            B[k][2] = ((q[k].z >> 4) & M4) | (((qh[k].z >> s1) & 0x01010101) << 4);
            B[k][3] = ((q[k].w >> 4) & M4) | (((qh[k].w >> s1) & 0x01010101) << 4);
        }
        accR<T, R, 32 * IT>(s, u, A, B, fA, gA, fB, gB, acc);
    }
}

// repacked Q6_K block (224 B): qlo[128] (q4k-style nibbles) | qhi[64] (8 B per unit) |
// meta[32]: for each 64-group p: sc[4p..4p+3], d (fp16), 2 pad bytes  -> one 8-byte load per unit
constexpr int Q6_BLK = 224;
template <int T, int IT, int R>
__device__ __forceinline__ void rows_q6k(const uint8_t * const (&rp)[R], const SX & s, float (&acc)[R][T]) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
#pragma unroll(IT > 8 ? (T >= 5 ? 1 : 4) : IT)
    for (int i = 0; i < IT; i++) {
        const int u = lane + 32 * i, b = u >> 3, p = (u >> 1) & 3, hh = u & 1;
        int4 q[R];
        int2 hb[R], meta[R];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            const uint8_t * blk = rp[k] + b * Q6_BLK;
            q[k] = ldw4(blk + 32 * p + 16 * hh);
            hb[k] = ldw2(blk + 128 + 8 * (u & 7));
            meta[k] = ldw2(blk + 192 + 8 * p);
        }
        int A[R][4], B[R][4];
        float fA[R], gA[R], fB[R], gB[R];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            const float d = __half2float(__ushort_as_half((unsigned short) (meta[k].y & 0xffff)));
            fA[k] = d * (float) (int8_t) (meta[k].x >> (8 * hh));
            fB[k] = d * (float) (int8_t) (meta[k].x >> (16 + 8 * hh));
            gA[k] = 32.0f * fA[k];
            gB[k] = 32.0f * fB[k];
            const unsigned hx = (unsigned) hb[k].x, hy = (unsigned) hb[k].y;
            A[k][0] = (q[k].x & M4) | (int) (((hx >> 0) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
            A[k][1] = (q[k].y & M4) | (int) (((hx >> 2) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
            A[k][2] = (q[k].z & M4) | (int) (((hx >> 4) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
            A[k][3] = (q[k].w & M4) | (int) (((hx >> 6) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
            B[k][0] = ((q[k].x >> 4) & M4) | (int) (((hy >> 0) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
            B[k][1] = ((q[k].y >> 4) & M4) | (int) (((hy >> 2) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
            B[k][2] = ((q[k].z >> 4) & M4) | (int) (((hy >> 4) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
            B[k][3] = ((q[k].w >> 4) & M4) | (int) (((hy >> 6) & 0x03030303u) << 4);
        }
        accR<T, R, 32 * IT>(s, u, A, B, fA, gA, fB, gB, acc);
    }
}

// repacked Q8_0: per unit 32 B (A 16 int8 | B 16 int8), scales per natural 32-block
template <int T, int IT, int R>
__device__ __forceinline__ void rows_q80(const uint8_t * const (&rp)[R], const __half * const (&dp)[R], const SX & s,
                                         float (&acc)[R][T]) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
#pragma unroll(IT > 8 ? (T >= 5 ? 1 : 4) : IT)
    for (int i = 0; i < IT; i++) {
        const int u = lane + 32 * i;
        int A[R][4], B[R][4];
        float fA[R], gA[R], fB[R], gB[R];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
            const int4 a = ldw4(rp[k] + 32 * u), bq = ldw4(rp[k] + 32 * u + 16);
            const int dd = ldw1(dp[k] + 2 * (u >> 1));
            const float2 df = __half22float2(*reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(&dd));
            A[k][0] = a.x; A[k][1] = a.y; A[k][2] = a.z; A[k][3] = a.w;
            B[k][0] = bq.x; B[k][1] = bq.y; B[k][2] = bq.z; B[k][3] = bq.w;
            fA[k] = df.x; fB[k] = df.y; gA[k] = 0.f; gB[k] = 0.f;
        }
        accR<T, R, 32 * IT>(s, u, A, B, fA, gA, fB, gB, acc);
    }
}

// Native IQ grids are decoded once per eight weights and reused for every token.
template <int T, int IT, int R, int TYPE = T_ANY>
__device__ __forceinline__ void rows_native_impl(const Mat & m, int row0, const SX & s, float (&acc)[R][T], int u0) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    // A reference parameter aliases the caller's stack. Keep the accumulation
    // inside this function in registers and only copy back after the K loop.
    float sum[R][T];
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r=0;r<R;r++) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int t=0;t<T;t++) sum[r][t]=acc[r][t];
    }
    if constexpr (T > 5 && R == 2 && (TYPE == 16 || TYPE == 17 || TYPE == 18 || TYPE == 21 || TYPE == 22 || TYPE == 23)) {
        if (cB.iq_paired) {
            iq_dp4a_dual<T, 32 * IT, TYPE, TYPE, true, false>(
                m.w + size_t(row0) * m.rb, m.w + size_t(row0 + 1) * m.rb,
                s.xa, s.xb, s.xm, sum[0], sum[1], u0 * 32);
            #pragma unroll
            for (int r=0;r<R;r++) {
                #pragma unroll
                for (int t=0;t<T;t++) acc[r][t]=sum[r][t];
            }
            return;
        }
    }
    #pragma unroll 1
    for (int i = 0; i < IT; i++) {
        const int u = lane + 32 * i;
        const int e0 = u0 * 32 + 256 * (u / 8) + 64 * ((u / 2) & 3) + 16 * (u & 1);
        if constexpr (TYPE == 18 || TYPE == 21 || TYPE == 23) {
            #pragma unroll
            for (int p = 0; p < 2; p++) {
                #pragma unroll
                for (int r = 0; r < R; r++) {
                    const Quant16 w = quant16<TYPE>(m.w + size_t(row0+r)*m.rb, e0+32*p);
                    #pragma unroll
                    for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) {
                        const int ix = t*(32*IT)+u;
                        const int4 x = p ? s.xb[ix] : s.xa[ix];
                        const float2 dx = __half22float2(*reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(&s.xm[ix].x));
                        const float d = p ? dx.y : dx.x;
                        const int dot = __dp4a(w.q.w,x.w,__dp4a(w.q.z,x.z,__dp4a(w.q.y,x.y,__dp4a(w.q.x,x.x,0))));
                        sum[r][t] += d*w.d*dot;
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
        #pragma unroll 1
        for (int f = 0; f < 4; f++) {
            #pragma unroll
            for (int r = 0; r < R; r++) {
                const Quant8 w = quant8(TYPE == T_ANY ? m.type : TYPE, m.w + size_t(row0 + r) * m.rb,
                                        e0 + (f / 2) * 32 + (f & 1) * 8,
                                        m.d ? m.d + size_t(row0 + r)*m.nd : nullptr);
                #pragma unroll
                for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) {
                    const int ix = t * (32 * IT) + u;
                    const int4 x = f < 2 ? s.xa[ix] : s.xb[ix];
                    const int a = (f & 1) ? x.z : x.x, b = (f & 1) ? x.w : x.y;
                    const float2 dx = __half22float2(*reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(&s.xm[ix].x));
                    const float d = f < 2 ? dx.x : dx.y;
                    const int xs = __dp4a(a, 0x01010101, __dp4a(b, 0x01010101, 0));
                    sum[r][t] += d * (w.d * __dp4a(w.q.x, a, __dp4a(w.q.y, b, 0)) + w.offset * xs);
                }
            }
        }
        }
    }
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r=0;r<R;r++) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int t=0;t<T;t++) acc[r][t]=sum[r][t];
    }
}

template <int T, int IT, int R, int TYPE = T_ANY>
__device__ __noinline__ void rows_native(const Mat & m, int row0, const SX & s, float (&acc)[R][T], int u0) {
    rows_native_impl<T, IT, R, TYPE>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
}

template <int T, int IT, int R, int TYPE>
__device__ __forceinline__ void native_rows(const Mat & m, int row0, const SX & s, float (&acc)[R][T], int u0) {
    if constexpr (T > 5 && R == 2 && TYPE != T_ANY) {
        if (cB.iq_inline) {
            rows_native_impl<T, IT, R, TYPE>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
            return;
        }
    }
    rows_native<T, IT, R, TYPE>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
}

// rows row0 .. row0+R-1 of m, weight type fixed at compile time
// u0: first unit of a K-slice (multiple of 32) when the activations in s hold only that slice
template <int TYPE>
__device__ __forceinline__ int unit_bytes(int u0) {
    return TYPE == T_Q4K ? (u0 >> 3) * 144 : TYPE == T_Q5K ? (u0 >> 3) * 176 : TYPE == T_Q6K ? (u0 >> 3) * Q6_BLK : 32 * u0;
}
template <int T, int IT, int R, int TYPE>
__device__ __forceinline__ void mrows(const Mat & m, int row0, const SX & s, float (&acc)[R][T], int u0 = 0) {
    if constexpr (TYPE == T_ANY) {
        switch (m.type) {
            case T_Q4K: mrows<T, IT, R, T_Q4K>(m, row0, s, acc, u0); break;
            case T_Q6K: mrows<T, IT, R, T_Q6K>(m, row0, s, acc, u0); break;
            case 16:
                if constexpr (T > 5) native_rows<T, IT, R, 16>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
                else rows_native<T, IT, R>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
                break;
            case 17:
                if constexpr (T > 5) native_rows<T, IT, R, 17>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
                else rows_native<T, IT, R>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
                break;
            case 22:
                if constexpr (T > 5) native_rows<T, IT, R, 22>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
                else rows_native<T, IT, R>(m, row0, s, acc, u0);
                break;
            case 18: native_rows<T, IT, R, 18>(m, row0, s, acc, u0); break;
            case 21: native_rows<T, IT, R, 21>(m, row0, s, acc, u0); break;
            case 23: native_rows<T, IT, R, 23>(m, row0, s, acc, u0); break;
            default: rows_native<T, IT, R>(m, row0, s, acc, u0); break;
        }
        return;
    }
    const uint8_t * rp[R];
    const __half * dp[R];
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < R; k++) {
        rp[k] = m.w + (size_t) (row0 + k) * m.rb + unit_bytes<TYPE>(u0);
        dp[k] = m.d + (size_t) (row0 + k) * m.nd + u0;
    }
    if constexpr (TYPE == T_Q4K) rows_q4k<T, IT, R>(rp, s, acc);
    else if constexpr (TYPE == T_Q5K) rows_q5k<T, IT, R>(rp, s, acc);
    else if constexpr (TYPE == T_Q6K) rows_q6k<T, IT, R>(rp, s, acc);
    else rows_q80<T, IT, R>(rp, dp, s, acc);
}

// Row pairs [lo, hi) of a single-matrix item range, distributed over all warps of the grid.
// out(row0, acc) receives the reduced sums for rows row0, row0 + 1.
template <int T, int IT, int TYPE, class Out>
__device__ __noinline__ void gemv_pairs(const Mat m, int lo, int hi, Out out) {
    const SX sx = make_sx(nullptr, 32 * IT); // activations already in shared memory
    const int W = nwarps(), gw = gwarp();
    const int first = lo + (((gw - lo) % W) + W) % W;
    for (int it = first; it < hi; it += W) {
        const int r = 2 * (it - lo);
        float a[2][T];
        zero_acc<T, 2>(a);
        mrows<T, IT, 2, TYPE>(m, r, sx, a);
        wsum_acc<T, 2>(a);
        out(r, a);
    }
}

// Q4_K or Q6_K matrix (the two types that occur for qkv / v projections)
template <int T, int IT, class Out>
__device__ __forceinline__ void gemv_pairs_46(const Mat & m, int lo, int hi, Out out) {
    gemv_pairs<T, IT, T_ANY>(m, lo, hi, out);
}


// Prefetch the first `bytes` of rows row0..row0+n-1 into L2 so the DRAM stream of a phase starts
// while its activations are still being loaded into shared memory.
__device__ __forceinline__ void pf_rows(const Mat & m, int row0, int n, int bytes) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
        const uint8_t * p = m.w + (size_t) (row0 + k) * m.rb;
        for (int off = lane * 128; off < bytes; off += 32 * 128) asm volatile("prefetch.global.L2 [%0];" ::"l"(p + off));
    }
}

// lanes 0-15 quantise 16-chunk c of row t, lanes 16-31 the same chunk of row t + 1 (if valid)
__device__ __forceinline__ void q16_store(const QBuf & q, int t, int c, float v, bool valid) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    float amax = fabsf(v);
    amax = fmaxf(amax, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, amax, 8));
    amax = fmaxf(amax, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, amax, 4));
    amax = fmaxf(amax, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, amax, 2));
    amax = fmaxf(amax, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, amax, 1));
    const float d = amax / 127.0f;
    const int qi = amax == 0.0f ? 0 : (int) roundf(v / d);
    int qs = qi;
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 8);
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 4);
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 2);
    qs += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, qs, 1);
    if (!valid) return;
    const int b = c >> 4, p = (c >> 2) & 3, part = (c >> 1) & 1, hh = c & 1, byte = lane & 15;
    const int gi = t * q.U + 8 * b + 2 * p + hh;
    (part ? q.xb : q.xa)[gi * 16 + byte] = (int8_t) qi;
    if (byte == 0) {
        reinterpret_cast<__half *>(q.meta + gi)[part] = __float2half_rn(d);
        reinterpret_cast<short *>(q.meta + gi)[2 + part] = (short) qs;
    }
}

template <int T>
__device__ __forceinline__ void wsum_all(float (&a)[T]) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) a[t] = wsum(a[t]);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// phases
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

__device__ __forceinline__ float dequant_q4k(const uint8_t * row, int e) {
    const int b = e >> 8, w = e & 255, sb = w >> 5, l = w & 31;
    const uint8_t * blk = row + b * 144;
    const float d = __half2float(*reinterpret_cast<const __half *>(blk));
    const float dmin = __half2float(*reinterpret_cast<const __half *>(blk + 2));
    const uint8_t * sc = blk + 4;
    int s, m;
    if (sb < 4) {
        s = sc[sb] & 63;
        m = sc[sb + 4] & 63;
    } else {
        s = (sc[sb + 4] & 0xF) | ((sc[sb - 4] >> 6) << 4);
        m = (sc[sb + 4] >> 4) | ((sc[sb] >> 6) << 4);
    }
    const uint8_t qb = blk[16 + 32 * (sb >> 1) + l];
    const int qv = (sb & 1) ? (qb >> 4) : (qb & 0xF);
    return d * (float) s * (float) qv - dmin * (float) m;
}

// token embedding rows -> x, plus quantised attn_norm input for layer 0
template <int T>
__device__ __noinline__ void embed_prologue() {
    const int t = blockIdx.x;
    if (t >= T) return;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    const int tok = ((volatile Ctl *) cB.ctl)->tok[t];
    const uint8_t * row = cW.embd + (size_t) tok * cW.embedding.rb;
    const float * w0 = cW.L[0].attn_norm;
    for (int j = warp; j < U_D; j += NW) {
        const int e = 32 * j + lane;
        const float v = native_value(cW.embedding.type, row, e, cW.embedding.d ? cW.embedding.d + size_t(tok) * cW.embedding.nd : nullptr);
        cB.x[t * D + e] = v;
        q32_store(cB.qn, t, j, v * w0[e], v * v, true);
    }
}

// Output rows (32 consecutive rows per block item) with optional residual add; the new
// residual value is re-quantised for the next RMS-normed consumer (weight wnext).
template <int T, int IT, int TYPE>
__device__ __noinline__ void resid32(const Mat W, const QBuf in, float * x, bool add, const float * wnext, uint8_t * /*sm*/) {
    uint8_t * const sm = smem;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    float * s_rs = misc(sm);
    float * s_res = misc(sm) + 8;
    if (blockIdx.x < D / 32) pf_rows(W, 32 * blockIdx.x + 2 * warp, 2, W.rb / IT);
    if (threadIdx.x < 4 && blockIdx.x + threadIdx.x * gridDim.x < D / 32) pf_l2(wnext + 32 * (blockIdx.x + threadIdx.x * gridDim.x));
    auto epilogue = [&](int j, float (&a)[2][T]) {
        wsum_acc<T, 2>(a);
        if (lane == 0) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) {
                s_res[t * 32 + 2 * warp] = a[0][t];
                s_res[t * 32 + 2 * warp + 1] = a[1][t];
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (warp < T) {
            const int t = warp, row = 32 * j + lane;
            float xv = s_res[t * 32 + lane];
            if (add) xv += __ldcg(x + t * D + row);
            const unsigned bad = __ballot_sync(0xffffffffu, !isfinite(xv));
            if (bad) {
                if (lane == __ffs(bad)-1) printf("[mk] nonfinite residual T=%d IT=%d type=%d row=%d n=%d\n", T, IT, W.type, row, cB.ctl->n);
                asm volatile("trap;");
            }
            x[t * D + row] = xv;
            q32_store(cB.qn, t, j, xv * wnext[row], xv * xv, true);
        }
        __syncthreads();
    };
    if constexpr (T > 5 && IT == 17) {
        if (cB.ffn_down_global) {
            SX sx;
            sx.xa = reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(in.xa);
            sx.xb = reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(in.xb);
            sx.xm = in.meta;
            sx.U = in.U;
            for (int j = blockIdx.x; j < D / 32; j += gridDim.x) {
                float a[2][T];
                zero_acc<T, 2>(a);
                mrows<T, IT, 2, TYPE>(W, 32 * j + 2 * warp, sx, a);
                epilogue(j, a);
            }
            return;
        }
    }
    if constexpr (T * 32 * IT > SM_XU) {
        // Five-token FFN down projections exceed the full activation staging
        // region. Process each output group across two K slices, keeping its
        // partial sums in registers instead of parking a second group in SM.
        constexpr int U0 = T <= 5 ? 32 * ((IT + 1) / 2) : 32 * (SM_XU / (32*T));
        constexpr int FULL = (32*IT)/U0, U1 = (32*IT)%U0;
        static_assert(T * U0 * 40 <= SM_MISC, "split FFN shared memory");
        auto sxh = [&](int US) {
            SX r;
            r.xa = reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(sm);
            r.xb = reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(sm + T * US * 16);
            r.xm = reinterpret_cast<uint2 *>(sm + 2 * T * US * 16);
            r.U = US;
            return r;
        };
        const SX s0 = sxh(U0), s1 = sxh(U1);
        for (int j = blockIdx.x; j < D / 32; j += gridDim.x) {
            float a[2][T];
            zero_acc<T, 2>(a);
            for (int k=0;k<FULL;k++) {
                fill_xs_part<T, U0>(in, k*U0, s0);
                mrows<T, U0 / 32, 2, TYPE>(W, 32*j+2*warp, s0, a, k*U0);
            }
            if constexpr (U1>0) {
                fill_xs_part<T, U1>(in, FULL*U0, s1);
                mrows<T, U1 / 32, 2, TYPE>(W, 32*j+2*warp, s1, a, FULL*U0);
            }
            epilogue(j, a);
        }
    } else {
        const SX sx = make_sx(sm, in.U);
        fill_xs<T>(in, 0, false, sx, s_rs);
        for (int j = blockIdx.x; j < D / 32; j += gridDim.x) {
            float a[2][T];
            zero_acc<T, 2>(a);
            mrows<T, IT, 2, TYPE>(W, 32 * j + 2 * warp, sx, a);
            epilogue(j, a);
        }
    }
}

// At large T, keep both IQ dots in registers and reuse each activation fragment.
// The per-dot accumulation order matches rows_native<...,1,TYPE>.
template <int T, int GT, int UT>
__device__ __noinline__ void ffn_gu_iq(const LayerW & W) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    const SX sx = make_sx(nullptr, U_D);
    const float * s_rs = misc(nullptr);
    for (int j = gwarp(); j < FF / 16; j += nwarps()) {
        float act[T];
#pragma unroll
        for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) act[t] = 0.f;
#pragma unroll 1
        for (int r = 0; r < 16; r++) {
            const int row = 16 * j + r;
            const uint8_t * gate_row = W.fg.w + size_t(row) * W.fg.rb;
            const uint8_t * up_row = W.fu.w + size_t(row) * W.fu.rb;
            float gate[T], up[T];
            if constexpr (GT == 16 || GT == 17 || GT == 22 || UT == 16 || UT == 17 || UT == 22) {
                if (cB.iq_paired) iq_dp4a_dual<T, U_D, GT, UT, true>(gate_row, up_row, sx.xa, sx.xb, sx.xm, gate, up);
                else iq_dp4a_dual<T, U_D, GT, UT>(gate_row, up_row, sx.xa, sx.xb, sx.xm, gate, up);
            } else iq_dp4a_dual<T, U_D, GT, UT>(gate_row, up_row, sx.xa, sx.xb, sx.xm, gate, up);
            wsum_all<T>(gate);
            wsum_all<T>(up);
            if (lane == r) {
#pragma unroll
                for (int t = 0; t < T; t++) {
                    act[t] = silu(gate[t] * s_rs[t]) * (up[t] * s_rs[t]);
#ifdef MK_PHASE_ONLY
                    if(cB.gu_probe){float * p=cB.gu_probe+(size_t(t)*FF+row)*5;p[0]=gate[t];p[1]=up[t];p[2]=gate[t]*s_rs[t];p[3]=up[t]*s_rs[t];p[4]=act[t];}
#endif
                }
            }
        }
#pragma unroll
        for (int t = 0; t < T; t += 2) {
            const float other = __shfl_sync(0xffffffffu, act[t + 1 < T ? t + 1 : t], lane & 15);
            q16_store(cB.qa, lane < 16 ? t : t + 1, j, lane < 16 ? act[t] : other, lane < 16 || t + 1 < T);
        }
    }
}

template<int T, int GT>
__device__ __forceinline__ bool ffn_gu_iq_up(const LayerW & W) {
    switch (W.fu.type) {
        case 16: ffn_gu_iq<T, GT, 16>(W); return true;
        case 17: ffn_gu_iq<T, GT, 17>(W); return true;
        case 18: ffn_gu_iq<T, GT, 18>(W); return true;
        case 21: ffn_gu_iq<T, GT, 21>(W); return true;
        case 22: ffn_gu_iq<T, GT, 22>(W); return true;
        case 23: ffn_gu_iq<T, GT, 23>(W); return true;
        default: return false;
    }
}

__device__ __noinline__ void ffn_gu_mma(const LayerW & W) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31, word = lane & 3;
    const SX sx = make_sx(nullptr, U_D);
    const float * s_rs = misc(nullptr);
    for (int j = gwarp(); j < FF / 16; j += nwarps()) {
        float values[2][2];
#pragma unroll
        for (int half = 0; half < 2; half++) {
            const int row = 16 * j + 8 * half;
            float gate[2], up[2];
            iq_mma_dual(W.fg.w + size_t(row) * W.fg.rb, W.fg.rb, W.fg.type,
                        W.fu.w + size_t(row) * W.fu.rb, W.fu.rb, W.fu.type,
                        sx.xa, sx.xb, sx.xm, U_D, gate, up);
#pragma unroll
            for (int t = 0; t < 2; t++) {
