    const int t = blockIdx.x, lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    for (int hh = warp; hh < NVH; hh += 8) {
        const float * o = cP.y2 + (size_t) t * VD + hh * 128;
        const float * z = cP.y1 + (size_t) t * VD + hh * 128;
        float v[4], ss = 0.f;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            v[i] = __ldg(o + lane + 32 * i);
            ss += v[i] * v[i];
        }
        const float rs = rsqrtf(wsum(ss) / 128.0f + EPS);
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const int c = lane + 32 * i;
            hstore32(v[i] * rs * __ldg(snorm + c) * silu(__ldg(z + c)), cP.xh + (size_t) t * VD + hh * 128 + 32 * i);
        }
    }
}

// attention q / k / v post-processing: RMS norms, RoPE, K / V -> selected KV cache, q -> qh
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) pk_attn_prep(int n0, int slot, const float * qn, const float * kn, int do_q) {
    const int t = blockIdx.x, pos = n0 + t, lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    const size_t ctx = cB.ctx;
    for (int it = warp; it < 32; it += 8) {
        if (it < NH && !do_q) continue;
        float v[8];
        if (it < NH) load8(cP.y0 + (size_t) t * QG + it * 512 + lane * 8, v);
        else load8(cP.y1 + (size_t) t * 2 * KVD + (it >= NH + NKV ? KVD : 0) + ((it - NH) % NKV) * HD + lane * 8, v);
        const bool isv = it >= NH + NKV;
        if (!isv) {
            const float * nw = it < NH ? qn : kn;
            float ss = 0.f;
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; k++) ss += v[k] * v[k];
            const float rs = rsqrtf(wsum(ss) / 256.0f + EPS);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; k++) v[k] *= rs * __ldg(nw + lane * 8 + k);
            rope8(v, lane, pos);
        }
        const float sc = it < NH ? 0.0625f : 1.0f;
        __half2 hv[4];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) hv[k] = __floats2half2_rn(v[2 * k] * sc, v[2 * k + 1] * sc);
        if (it < NH) {
            *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(cP.qh + ((size_t) t * NH + it) * HD + lane * 8) = *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(hv);
        } else {
            const size_t row = ((size_t) slot * NKV + (it - NH) % NKV) * ctx + pos;
            if (cB.kv_mode == 2) kv_store<2>(isv ? cB.vc : cB.kc, isv ? cB.vsc : cB.ksc, row, v);
            else if (cB.kv_mode == 1) kv_store_q8(static_cast<int8_t *>(isv ? cB.vc : cB.kc) + row * HD, (isv ? cB.vsc : cB.ksc) + row * (HD / 32), v);
            else *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(static_cast<__half *>(isv ? cB.vc : cB.kc) + row * HD + lane * 8) = *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(hv);
        }
    }
}

// Causal flash attention over the cache for the chunk's queries.  CTA = (16 query positions, kv
// head); warp = one of the 6 query heads of the group (16 rows).  K / V tiles of 32 positions in
// shared memory, S = Q K^T and O += P V on tensor cores (fp16 operands, fp32 accumulation).
constexpr int AT_Q = 16, AT_K = 32, AT_LD = HD + 8;
constexpr int AT_SMEM = (6 * AT_Q + 2 * AT_K) * AT_LD * 2; // Q, K/V tile
template <int MODE>
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(192) pk_attn(int T, int n0, int slot) {
    extern __shared__ __align__(16) uint8_t asm_[];
    __half * sQ = reinterpret_cast<__half *>(asm_); // [96][AT_LD]
    __half * sK = sQ + 6 * AT_Q * AT_LD;            // [32][AT_LD]
    __half * sV = sK + AT_K * AT_LD;                // [32][AT_LD]
    const int tid = threadIdx.x, lane = tid & 31, warp = tid >> 5, g8 = lane >> 2, t4 = lane & 3;
    const int t0 = blockIdx.x * AT_Q, g = blockIdx.y;
    const size_t ctx = cB.ctx;
    const size_t row0 = ((size_t) slot * NKV + g) * ctx;
    const __half * Kc = MODE == 0 ? static_cast<const __half *>(cB.kc) + row0 * HD : nullptr;
    const __half * Vc = MODE == 0 ? static_cast<const __half *>(cB.vc) + row0 * HD : nullptr;
    const int nq = min(AT_Q, T - t0);
    for (int i = tid; i < 6 * AT_Q * 32; i += 192) { // 96 rows x 32 chunks of 16 B
        const int r = i >> 5, c = i & 31, hq = r / AT_Q, tt = r % AT_Q;
        __half * d = sQ + r * AT_LD + 8 * c;
        if (tt < nq) cp_async16(d, cP.qh + ((size_t) (t0 + tt) * NH + g * 6 + hq) * HD + 8 * c);
        else *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(d) = make_int4(0, 0, 0, 0);
    }
    pf::cpa_commit();
    const int qpos0 = n0 + t0, pmax = qpos0 + nq - 1;
    float o[32][4];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++) o[i][0] = o[i][1] = o[i][2] = o[i][3] = 0.f;
    float m0 = -INFINITY, m1 = -INFINITY, l0 = 0.f, l1 = 0.f; // rows g8, g8 + 8
    const int qr0 = qpos0 + g8, qr1 = qpos0 + g8 + 8;
    const __half * qa = sQ + (warp * AT_Q + g8) * AT_LD + 2 * t4;
    for (int kb = 0; kb <= pmax; kb += AT_K) {
        const int np = min(AT_K, pmax + 1 - kb);
        if constexpr (MODE != 0) { // packed Q8/Q4 K/V tile -> fp16 in shared memory (all loads issued before the first conversion)
            constexpr int PER = (2 * AT_K * 32 + 191) / 192;
            uint2 q[PER];
            float d[PER];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < PER; k++) {
                const int i = tid + 192 * k, which = i / (AT_K * 32), r = (i >> 5) % AT_K, c = i & 31;
                q[k] = make_uint2(0, 0);
                d[k] = 0.f;
                if (i < 2 * AT_K * 32 && r < np) {
                    const size_t row = row0 + kb + r;
                    if constexpr (MODE == 2) q[k].x = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(static_cast<const uint8_t *>(which ? cB.vc : cB.kc) + row * (HD / 2)) + c);
                    else q[k] = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const uint2 *>(static_cast<const int8_t *>(which ? cB.vc : cB.kc) + row * HD) + c);
                    d[k] = __half2float((which ? cB.vsc : cB.ksc)[row * (HD / 32) + (c >> 2)]);
                }
            }
            pf::cpa_wait<0>(); // Q rows (first tile)
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < PER; k++) {
                const int i = tid + 192 * k, which = i / (AT_K * 32), r = (i >> 5) % AT_K, c = i & 31;
                if (i >= 2 * AT_K * 32) break;
                __half2 h[4];
                if constexpr (MODE == 2) q4_to_f16(q[k].x, d[k], h);
                else q8_to_f16(q[k], d[k], h);
                *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>((which ? sV : sK) + r * AT_LD + 8 * c) = *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(h);
            }
        } else {
            for (int i = tid; i < AT_K * 32; i += 192) {
                const int p = i >> 5, c = i & 31;
                if (p < np) {
                    cp_async16(sK + p * AT_LD + 8 * c, Kc + (size_t) (kb + p) * HD + 8 * c);
                    cp_async16(sV + p * AT_LD + 8 * c, Vc + (size_t) (kb + p) * HD + 8 * c);
                } else {
                    *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(sK + p * AT_LD + 8 * c) = make_int4(0, 0, 0, 0);
                    *reinterpret_cast<int4 *>(sV + p * AT_LD + 8 * c) = make_int4(0, 0, 0, 0);
                }
            }
            pf::cpa_commit();
            pf::cpa_wait<0>();
        }
        __syncthreads();
        float s[4][4];
#pragma unroll
        for (int ni = 0; ni < 4; ni++) s[ni][0] = s[ni][1] = s[ni][2] = s[ni][3] = 0.f;
#pragma unroll
        for (int ks = 0; ks < HD / 16; ks++) {
            const unsigned a0 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(qa + 16 * ks);
            const unsigned a1 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(qa + 8 * AT_LD + 16 * ks);
            const unsigned a2 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(qa + 16 * ks + 8);
            const unsigned a3 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(qa + 8 * AT_LD + 16 * ks + 8);
#pragma unroll
            for (int ni = 0; ni < 4; ni++) {
                const __half * kp = sK + (8 * ni + g8) * AT_LD + 2 * t4 + 16 * ks;
                mma16816(s[ni], a0, a1, a2, a3, *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(kp),
                         *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(kp + 8));
            }
        }
        // causal mask + online softmax (rows g8 and g8 + 8; a row's 4 lanes share it)
        float mx0 = -INFINITY, mx1 = -INFINITY;
#pragma unroll
        for (int ni = 0; ni < 4; ni++) {
            const int p = kb + 8 * ni + 2 * t4;
            if (p > qr0) s[ni][0] = -INFINITY;
            if (p + 1 > qr0) s[ni][1] = -INFINITY;
            if (p > qr1) s[ni][2] = -INFINITY;
            if (p + 1 > qr1) s[ni][3] = -INFINITY;
            mx0 = fmaxf(mx0, fmaxf(s[ni][0], s[ni][1]));
            mx1 = fmaxf(mx1, fmaxf(s[ni][2], s[ni][3]));
        }
        mx0 = fmaxf(mx0, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, mx0, 1));
        mx0 = fmaxf(mx0, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, mx0, 2));
        mx1 = fmaxf(mx1, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, mx1, 1));
        mx1 = fmaxf(mx1, __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, mx1, 2));
        const float mn0 = fmaxf(m0, mx0), mn1 = fmaxf(m1, mx1);
        const float al0 = mn0 == -INFINITY ? 1.f : expf(m0 - mn0), al1 = mn1 == -INFINITY ? 1.f : expf(m1 - mn1);
        float ps0 = 0.f, ps1 = 0.f;
        unsigned pa[4][2]; // P as fp16 pairs: [n8 tile][row g8 / g8 + 8]
#pragma unroll
        for (int ni = 0; ni < 4; ni++) {
            const float p0 = mn0 == -INFINITY ? 0.f : expf(s[ni][0] - mn0);
            const float p1 = mn0 == -INFINITY ? 0.f : expf(s[ni][1] - mn0);
            const float p2 = mn1 == -INFINITY ? 0.f : expf(s[ni][2] - mn1);
            const float p3 = mn1 == -INFINITY ? 0.f : expf(s[ni][3] - mn1);
            ps0 += p0 + p1;
            ps1 += p2 + p3;
            __half2 h01 = __floats2half2_rn(p0, p1), h23 = __floats2half2_rn(p2, p3);
            pa[ni][0] = *reinterpret_cast<unsigned *>(&h01);
            pa[ni][1] = *reinterpret_cast<unsigned *>(&h23);
        }
        ps0 += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, ps0, 1);
        ps0 += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, ps0, 2);
        ps1 += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, ps1, 1);
        ps1 += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, ps1, 2);
        l0 = l0 * al0 + ps0;
        l1 = l1 * al1 + ps1;
        m0 = mn0;
        m1 = mn1;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
            o[i][0] *= al0;
            o[i][1] *= al0;
            o[i][2] *= al1;
            o[i][3] *= al1;
        }
        // O += P V: k = positions (2 steps of 16), n = 32 tiles of 8 dims
#pragma unroll
        for (int ks = 0; ks < AT_K / 16; ks++) {
            const unsigned a0 = pa[2 * ks][0], a1 = pa[2 * ks][1], a2 = pa[2 * ks + 1][0], a3 = pa[2 * ks + 1][1];
#pragma unroll
            for (int dt2 = 0; dt2 < 16; dt2++) { // two 8-dim tiles per ldmatrix.x4
                const __half * vb = sV + (16 * ks + (lane & 15)) * AT_LD + 16 * dt2 + (lane >> 4) * 8;
                unsigned b0, b1, b2, b3;
                asm volatile("ldmatrix.sync.aligned.m8n8.x4.trans.shared.b16 {%0,%1,%2,%3}, [%4];"
                             : "=r"(b0), "=r"(b1), "=r"(b2), "=r"(b3)
                             : "r"((unsigned) __cvta_generic_to_shared(vb)));
                mma16816(o[2 * dt2], a0, a1, a2, a3, b0, b1);
                mma16816(o[2 * dt2 + 1], a0, a1, a2, a3, b2, b3);
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    const float il0 = l0 > 0.f ? 1.f / l0 : 0.f, il1 = l1 > 0.f ? 1.f / l1 : 0.f;
    const int hq = g * 6 + warp;
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
        const int d = 8 * i + 2 * t4;
        if (g8 < nq) {
            float * dst = cP.y2 + (size_t) (t0 + g8) * VD + hq * HD + d;
            dst[0] = o[i][0] * il0;
            dst[1] = o[i][1] * il0;
        }
        if (g8 + 8 < nq) {
            float * dst = cP.y2 + (size_t) (t0 + g8 + 8) * VD + hq * HD + d;
            dst[0] = o[i][2] * il1;
            dst[1] = o[i][3] * il1;
        }
    }
}

// attention output * sigmoid(gate) -> fp16 xh (K = VD)
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) pk_attn_out() {
    const int t = blockIdx.x, lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    for (int j = warp; j < VD / 32; j += 8) {
        const int e = 32 * j + lane, h = e / HD, d = e % HD;
        const float gate = __ldg(cP.y0 + (size_t) t * QG + h * 512 + 256 + d);
        hstore32(__ldg(cP.y2 + (size_t) t * VD + e) / (1.0f + expf(-gate)), cP.xh + (size_t) t * VD + 32 * j);
    }
}

// final RMS norm -> h rows (h[t + 1] = position n0 + t) and the decode h ring (last 16 positions)
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) pk_final_h(int T, int n0) {
    __shared__ float red[8];
    const int t = blockIdx.x;
    const float * x = cP.x + (size_t) t * D;
    float ss = 0.f;
    for (int e = threadIdx.x; e < D; e += 256) {
        const float v = __ldg(x + e);
        ss += v * v;
    }
    const float rs = rsqrtf(block_sum256(ss, red) / (float) D + EPS);
    const bool ring = t >= T - H_RING;
    for (int e = threadIdx.x; e < D; e += 256) {
        const float v = __ldg(x + e) * rs * __ldg(cW.onorm + e);
        cP.h[(size_t) (t + 1) * D + e] = v;
        if (ring) cB.h_hist[(size_t) ((n0 + t) % H_RING) * D + e] = v;
    }
}

// MTP input rows: concat(enorm(embed(tok_p)), hnorm(h_{p-1})) -> fp16 xh (K = 2 D)
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) pk_mtp_in() {
    __shared__ float se[D];
    __shared__ float red[8];
    const int t = blockIdx.x, lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    const uint8_t * row = cW.embedding.w + (size_t) cP.tok[t] * cW.embedding.rb;
    const float * hp = cP.h + (size_t) t * D;
    float ae = 0.f, ah = 0.f;
    for (int e = threadIdx.x; e < D; e += 256) {
        const float v = native_value(cW.embedding.type, row, e, cW.embedding.d ? cW.embedding.d + (size_t) cP.tok[t] * cW.embedding.nd : nullptr), hv = __ldg(hp + e);
        se[e] = v;
        ae += v * v;
        ah += hv * hv;
    }
    const float rse = rsqrtf(block_sum256(ae, red) / (float) D + EPS);
    const float rsh = rsqrtf(block_sum256(ah, red) / (float) D + EPS);
    for (int j = warp; j < 2 * D / 32; j += 8) {
        const int e = 32 * (j % (D / 32)) + lane;
        const float v = j < D / 32 ? se[e] * rse * __ldg(cW.enorm + e) : __ldg(hp + e) * rsh * __ldg(cW.hnorm + e);
        hstore32(v, cP.xh + (size_t) t * 2 * D + 32 * j);
    }
}

// The packed prefill GEMM handles complete 128-row tiles. Condensed Q4 heads
// need a bounded tail kernel for scoring; decode already processes row pairs.
__global__ void pk_q4_head_tail(Mat m, const __half * x, int ntok, float * y, int ldy, int row0) {
    const int row=row0+blockIdx.x, t=blockIdx.y, lane=threadIdx.x&31, warp=threadIdx.x>>5;
    if(row>=m.N || t>=ntok)return;
    float acc=0;
    for(int e=threadIdx.x;e<m.K;e+=256) {
        const float w=__half2float(__float2half_rn(dequant_q4k_ldg(m.w+size_t(row)*m.rb,e)));
        const float a=__half2float(__ldg(x+size_t(t)*m.K+32*(e/32)+pf::hperm(e%32)));
        acc=fmaf(w,a,acc);
    }
    acc=wsum(acc);
    __shared__ float sums[8];
    if(lane==0)sums[warp]=acc;
    __syncthreads();
    acc=threadIdx.x<8?sums[threadIdx.x]:0;
    acc=wsum(acc);
    if(threadIdx.x==0)y[size_t(t)*ldy+row]=acc;
}

// argmax over logits rows y0[t][0..cW.head.N) (scoring)
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) pk_argmax(int t0) {
    __shared__ float bv[8];
    __shared__ int bi[8];
    const int t = blockIdx.x, lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    const float * l = cP.y0 + (size_t) t * cW.head.N;
    float b = -INFINITY;
    int ix = 0;
    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < cW.head.N; i += 256)
        if (__ldg(l + i) > b) { b = __ldg(l + i); ix = i; }
#pragma unroll
    for (int o = 16; o > 0; o >>= 1) {
        const float ob = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, b, o);
        const int oi = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, ix, o);
        if (ob > b || (ob == b && oi < ix)) { b = ob; ix = oi; }
    }
    if (lane == 0) { bv[warp] = b; bi[warp] = ix; }
    __syncthreads();
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
        for (int w = 1; w < 8; w++)
            if (bv[w] > b || (bv[w] == b && bi[w] < ix)) { b = bv[w]; ix = bi[w]; }
        cP.amax[t0 + t] = ix;
    }
}

// Bounded workspace fallback for IQ / Q2_K prefill. Weights are dequantized one
// matrix at a time; FP16 tensor-core inputs accumulate in FP32, as in mmh.
__global__ void pk_native_weights(Mat m, __half * dst) {
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < size_t(m.N) * m.K / 8; i += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const size_t e0=8*i;
        const int r=e0/m.K, e=e0%m.K;
        const Quant8 w=quant8(m.type,m.w+size_t(r)*m.rb,e,m.d?m.d+size_t(r)*m.nd:nullptr);
        unsigned h[4];
        #pragma unroll
        for(int j=0;j<4;j++) {
            const unsigned q=unsigned(j<2?w.q.x:w.q.y) >> (16*(j%2));
            const __half2 pair = __floats2half2_rn(w.d*int8_t(q)+w.offset, w.d*int8_t(q>>8)+w.offset);
            h[j] = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(&pair);
        }
        reinterpret_cast<uint4 *>(dst)[i]=make_uint4(h[0],h[1],h[2],h[3]);
    }
}
template <int TYPE>
__global__ void pk_native_weights16(Mat m, __half * dst) {
    for(size_t i=size_t(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
        i<size_t(m.N)*m.K/16; i+=size_t(gridDim.x)*blockDim.x) {
        const size_t e0=16*i;
        const Quant16 w=quant16<TYPE>(m.w+(e0/m.K)*m.rb, e0%m.K);
        const int * words=reinterpret_cast<const int *>(&w.q);
        unsigned h[8];
        #pragma unroll
        for(int j=0;j<8;j++) {
            const unsigned q=unsigned(words[j/2])>>(16*(j%2));
            const __half2 pair=__floats2half2_rn(w.d*int8_t(q),w.d*int8_t(q>>8));
            h[j]=*reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(&pair);
        }
        reinterpret_cast<uint4 *>(dst)[2*i]=make_uint4(h[0],h[1],h[2],h[3]);
        reinterpret_cast<uint4 *>(dst)[2*i+1]=make_uint4(h[4],h[5],h[6],h[7]);
    }
}
__global__ void pk_native_input(const __half * src, __half * dst, int K, int T) {
    for (int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < K*T; i += gridDim.x*blockDim.x)
        dst[i] = src[(i/K)*K + 32*((i%K)/32) + pf::hperm(i%32)];
}
__global__ void pk_native_glu(int T) {
    for (int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < FF*T; i += gridDim.x*blockDim.x) {
        const float g = cP.y0[i], u = cP.y1[i];
        const float v = g / (1.f + expf(-g)) * u;
        cP.xh2[(i/FF)*FF + 32*((i%FF)/32) + pf::hperm(i%32)] =
            __float2half_rn(fminf(fmaxf(v, -65504.f), 65504.f));
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: megakernel/prefill_gemm.cuh
// Batched (prefill) GEMM on fp16 tensor cores against the k-quant weights in their stored layout.
//
//   Y[tok][row] (+)= sum_k W[row][k] * X[tok][k]
//
// X is fp16 [tok][K] with the elements of every 16-group permuted (hperm) so that the 4-byte weight
// words give m16n8k16 A fragments directly.  W stays Q4_K / Q5_K (GGUF layout), Q6_K (224-byte
// repack, see qwen38_mk.cu) or Q8_0 (unit repack) and is dequantised to fp16 in registers.
// Tile: 128 weight rows x 128 tokens per CTA, 8 warps of 16 rows x 128 tokens.  K is staged 64 at a
// time (one 64-group of a super-block: two 32-element sub-blocks) through a cp.async pipeline with
// one block barrier per stage; per-row sub-block scales are decoded one stage ahead.  Products
// accumulate in fp16 over two stages (k = 128) and are then added to fp32 accumulators.
#pragma once

#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <type_traits>

namespace pf {

constexpr int BM = 128, BN = 128, BK = 64;
constexpr int TQ4K = 12, TQ5K = 13, TQ6K = 14, TQ80 = 8;

struct WMat { // device view of one weight matrix (mirrors Mat in qwen38_mk.cu)
    const uint8_t * w;
    const __half * d;
    int type, K, N, rb, nd;
};

// Raw bytes of one stage (64-group p of super-block sb) per weight row, copied as 16 B chunks:
//   Q4_K: [hdr 16 | qs 32]                    src: blk + {0, 16 + 32p}
//   Q5_K: [hdr 16 | qh 32 | qs 32]            src: blk + {0, 16, 32, 48 + 32p, 64 + 32p}
//   Q6_K: [qlo 32 | qhi 16 | meta 8]          src: blk + {32p, 16 + 32p, 128 + 16p}, 8 B at 192 + 8p
//   Q8_0: [units 64 | d 4]                    src: blk + {64p + 16c}, 4 B of d at 2 * (2 * st)
template <int TYPE> struct WT;
template <> struct WT<TQ4K> { static constexpr int BLK = 144, NCH = 3; };
template <> struct WT<TQ5K> { static constexpr int BLK = 176, NCH = 5; };
template <> struct WT<TQ6K> { static constexpr int BLK = 224, NCH = 3; };
template <> struct WT<TQ80> { static constexpr int BLK = 256, NCH = 4; };

template <int TYPE> __device__ __forceinline__ void chunk_src(int c, int & off, int & inc) {
    if constexpr (TYPE == TQ4K) { off = c == 0 ? 0 : 16 * c; inc = c == 0 ? 0 : 32; }
    else if constexpr (TYPE == TQ5K) { off = c < 3 ? 16 * c : 48 + 16 * (c - 3); inc = c < 3 ? 0 : 32; }
    else if constexpr (TYPE == TQ6K) { off = c < 2 ? 16 * c : 128; inc = c < 2 ? 32 : 16; }
    else { off = 16 * c; inc = 64; }
}

__device__ __forceinline__ void cpa16(unsigned s, const void * g) {
    asm volatile("cp.async.cg.shared.global [%0], [%1], 16;" ::"r"(s), "l"(g) : "memory");
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void cpa8(unsigned s, const void * g) {
    asm volatile("cp.async.ca.shared.global [%0], [%1], 8;" ::"r"(s), "l"(g) : "memory");
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void cpa4(unsigned s, const void * g) {
    asm volatile("cp.async.ca.shared.global [%0], [%1], 4;" ::"r"(s), "l"(g) : "memory");
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void cpa_commit() { asm volatile("cp.async.commit_group;" ::: "memory"); }
template <int N> __device__ __forceinline__ void cpa_wait() { asm volatile("cp.async.wait_group %0;" ::"n"(N) : "memory"); }

__device__ __forceinline__ void ldsm_x4(unsigned (&r)[4], unsigned addr) {
    asm volatile("ldmatrix.sync.aligned.m8n8.x4.shared.b16 {%0,%1,%2,%3}, [%4];"
                 : "=r"(r[0]), "=r"(r[1]), "=r"(r[2]), "=r"(r[3])
                 : "r"(addr));
}

constexpr unsigned M4 = 0x0F0F0F0Fu;

enum { OUT_STORE = 0, OUT_ADD = 1 };

struct Out {
    float * y; // y[tok * ldy + row]
    int ldy;
    int mode;
    __half * yh; // fused gate / up (mmh<.., true>): yh[tok * ldy + row] = silu(gate) * up, fp16 in hperm order
};

// Element e of a 16-group sits at slot hperm(e): 4 t + {0, 1} -> 2 t + {0, 1}, 4 t + {2, 3} ->
// 8 + 2 t + {0, 1}.  With that order a weight word (elements 4 t4 .. 4 t4 + 3) gives the A fragment
// registers a0 / a1 = (e0, e1) and a2 / a3 = (e2, e3) of an m16n8k16 MMA.
__host__ __device__ constexpr int hperm(int e) {
    return (e & ~15) | ((((e >> 1) & 1) << 2 | ((e >> 2) & 3)) << 1) | (e & 1);
}

template <int TYPE> struct HT;
// SR: smem bytes per weight row; SEP: fp16 scales in a separate double buffer (else in the row:
// Q6_K rows are 64 B, chunks XOR-swizzled by (row / 2) % 4, scales in the meta chunk's spare 8 B)
template <> struct HT<TQ4K> { static constexpr int SR = 48, NS = 4; static constexpr bool SEP = true; };
template <> struct HT<TQ5K> { static constexpr int SR = 80, NS = 3; static constexpr bool SEP = true; };
template <> struct HT<TQ6K> { static constexpr int SR = 64, NS = 4; static constexpr bool SEP = false; };
template <> struct HT<TQ80> { static constexpr int SR = 80, NS = 3; static constexpr bool SEP = true; };
constexpr int HCH = 2; // stages accumulated in fp16 before adding to the fp32 accumulators
constexpr int HMI = 1, HWR = 16 * HMI, HNWM = BM / HWR, HWT = BN / (8 / HNWM), HNI = HWT / 8; // warp tiles
constexpr int HM_X = BN * 128; // activation tile per stage: 128 tokens x 64 halves, 16 B chunks XOR-swizzled
template <int TYPE> __host__ __device__ constexpr int hm_stage() { return HT<TYPE>::SR * BM + HM_X; }
template <int TYPE> __host__ __device__ constexpr int hm_smem() { return HT<TYPE>::NS * hm_stage<TYPE>() + (HT<TYPE>::SEP ? 2 * 2 * BM * 8 : 0); }
static_assert(BN * (BM + 4) * 4 <= hm_smem<TQ5K>() && BN * (BM + 4) * 4 <= hm_smem<TQ4K>(), "epilogue tile");

__device__ __forceinline__ void hmma16(unsigned (&d)[2], const unsigned (&a)[4], unsigned b0, unsigned b1) {
    asm volatile("mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16.row.col.f16.f16.f16.f16 {%0,%1}, {%2,%3,%4,%5}, {%6,%7}, {%0,%1};"
                 : "+r"(d[0]), "+r"(d[1])
                 : "r"(a[0]), "r"(a[1]), "r"(a[2]), "r"(a[3]), "r"(b0), "r"(b1));
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void hmma16z(unsigned (&d)[2], const unsigned (&a)[4], unsigned b0, unsigned b1) {
    asm volatile("mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16.row.col.f16.f16.f16.f16 {%0,%1}, {%2,%3,%4,%5}, {%6,%7}, {%8,%8};"
                 : "=r"(d[0]), "=r"(d[1])
                 : "r"(a[0]), "r"(a[1]), "r"(a[2]), "r"(a[3]), "r"(b0), "r"(b1), "r"(0));
}
__device__ __forceinline__ __half2 u2h(unsigned u) { return *reinterpret_cast<__half2 *>(&u); }
__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned h2u(__half2 h) { return *reinterpret_cast<unsigned *>(&h); }

// GLU: W2 holds the up matrix (same type and shape as W = gate); the CTA computes 64 gate rows
// (warps 0-3) and the same 64 up rows (warps 4-7) and writes silu(gate) * up as fp16.
template <int TYPE, bool GLU = false>
__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256, 1) mmh(WMat W, const __half * X, int ntok, Out O, const uint8_t * W2 = nullptr) {
    static_assert(!GLU || TYPE == TQ4K, "fused gate / up is only built for Q4_K");
    extern __shared__ __align__(16) uint8_t sm[];
    constexpr int BLK = WT<TYPE>::BLK, NCH = WT<TYPE>::NCH, SR = HT<TYPE>::SR, NSH = HT<TYPE>::NS;
    constexpr int STG = hm_stage<TYPE>();
    const int tid = threadIdx.x, lane = tid & 31, warp = tid >> 5, g = lane >> 2, t4 = lane & 3;
    const int wm = warp % HNWM, wn = warp / HNWM; // warp tile: rows HWR wm .., tokens HWT wn ..
    const int ntt = gridDim.y;
    const int lin = blockIdx.y * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x;
    const int row0 = (lin / ntt) * (GLU ? BM / 2 : BM), tok0 = (lin % ntt) * BN;
    const unsigned sbase = (unsigned) __cvta_generic_to_shared(sm);
    uint2 * sws = reinterpret_cast<uint2 *>(sm + NSH * STG); // [2][2 j][BM] fp16 scale pairs

    constexpr int WCH = BM * NCH, WIT = (WCH + 255) / 256;
    const uint8_t * wsrc[WIT];
    int winc[WIT];
    unsigned wdst[WIT];
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < WIT; k++) {
        const int i = tid + k * 256, r = i / NCH, c = i % NCH;
        int off, inc;
        chunk_src<TYPE>(c, off, inc);
        const int rr = i < WCH ? r : 0;
        wsrc[k] = GLU ? (rr < BM / 2 ? W.w : W2) + (size_t) (row0 + (rr & (BM / 2 - 1))) * W.rb + off
                      : W.w + (size_t) (row0 + rr) * W.rb + off;
        winc[k] = inc;
        wdst[k] = r * SR + 16 * (TYPE == TQ6K ? c ^ ((r >> 1) & 3) : c);
    }
    const uint8_t * esrc = W.w + (size_t) (row0 + (tid & (BM - 1))) * W.rb + 192;
    const __half * dsrc = W.d + (size_t) (row0 + (tid & (BM - 1))) * W.nd;
    const unsigned edst = (tid & (BM - 1)) * SR + (TYPE == TQ6K ? 16 * (3 ^ (((tid & (BM - 1)) >> 1) & 3)) : 64);
    const __half * xsrc[4];
    unsigned xdst[4];
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
        const int i = tid + k * 256, t = i >> 3, c = i & 7;
        xsrc[k] = X + (size_t) (tok0 + t) * W.K + 8 * c;
        xdst[k] = SR * BM + t * 128 + ((c ^ (t & 7)) << 4);
    }

    auto load = [&](int st) {
        const unsigned s = sbase + (st % NSH) * STG;
        const int sb = st >> 2, p = st & 3;
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < WIT; k++)
            if (WCH % 256 == 0 || tid + k * 256 < WCH) cpa16(s + wdst[k], wsrc[k] + sb * BLK + p * winc[k]);
        if constexpr (TYPE == TQ6K) {
            if (tid < BM) cpa8(s + edst, esrc + sb * BLK + 8 * p);
        } else if constexpr (TYPE == TQ80) {
            if (tid < BM) cpa4(s + edst, dsrc + 2 * st);
        }
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) cpa16(s + xdst[k], xsrc[k] + BK * st);
    };

    // fp16 scales of stage st -> sws[st & 1][j][row] (thread = (row, j)):
    //   Q4_K / Q5_K: (S, S, -M, -M);  Q6_K: (S_lo, S_lo, S_hi, S_hi);  Q8_0: (d, d, d, d)
    auto scales = [&](int st) {
        const uint8_t * row = sm + (st % NSH) * STG + (tid >> 1) * SR;
        const int j = tid & 1, p = st & 3;
        __half2 a, b;
        if constexpr (TYPE == TQ4K || TYPE == TQ5K) {
            const int4 hd = *reinterpret_cast<const int4 *>(row);
            const float2 dm = __half22float2(*reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(&hd.x));
            const int sh = (p & 1) * 16 + 8 * j;
            const unsigned y = (unsigned) hd.y >> sh, z = (unsigned) hd.z >> sh, w = (unsigned) hd.w >> sh;
            unsigned sc, m;
            if (p < 2) {
                sc = y & 63;
                m = z & 63;
            } else {
                sc = (w & 0xF) | ((y >> 2) & 0x30);
                m = ((w >> 4) & 0xF) | ((z >> 2) & 0x30);
            }
            a = __float2half2_rn(dm.x * (float) sc);
            b = __float2half2_rn(-dm.y * (float) m);
        } else if constexpr (TYPE == TQ6K) {
            uint8_t * mrow = const_cast<uint8_t *>(row) + 16 * (3 ^ (((tid >> 1) >> 1) & 3));
            const int2 meta = *reinterpret_cast<const int2 *>(mrow);
            const float d = __half2float(__ushort_as_half((unsigned short) (meta.y & 0xffff)));
            const unsigned sp = (unsigned) meta.x >> (16 * j);
            *reinterpret_cast<__half2 *>(mrow + 8 + 4 * j) =
                __floats2half2_rn(d * (float) (int8_t) (sp & 0xff), d * (float) (int8_t) ((sp >> 8) & 0xff));
            return;
        } else {
            const __half dd = *reinterpret_cast<const __half *>(row + 64 + 2 * j);
            a = b = __half2half2(dd);
        }
        if constexpr (HT<TYPE>::SEP) sws[(st & 1) * 2 * BM + j * BM + (tid >> 1)] = make_uint2(h2u(a), h2u(b));
    };

    float acc[HMI][HNI][4];
#pragma unroll
    for (int m = 0; m < HMI; m++)
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < HNI; i++) acc[m][i][0] = acc[m][i][1] = acc[m][i][2] = acc[m][i][3] = 0.f;

    const int nst = W.K / BK;
#pragma unroll
    for (int s = 0; s < NSH - 1; s++) {
        if (s < nst) load(s);
        cpa_commit();
    }
    cpa_wait<NSH - 2>();
    __syncthreads();
    scales(0);

    // ldmatrix rows: lanes 0-7 / 8-15 / 16-23 / 24-31 -> tokens +0..7 (slots 0-7) / +0..7 (slots 8-15)
    // / +8..15 (slots 0-7) / +8..15 (slots 8-15) of a 16-token pair of n8 tiles
    const unsigned xl = SR * BM + (HWT * wn + 8 * (lane >> 4) + (lane & 7)) * 128;
    unsigned xo[4];
#pragma unroll
    for (int G = 0; G < 4; G++) xo[G] = xl + (((2 * G + ((lane >> 3) & 1)) ^ (lane & 7)) << 4);

    // integer-valued fp16 weights (offset removed) of a stage: vq[mi][j][kk][0..3], A-fragment order
    unsigned vq[HMI][2][2][4];
    auto rawq = [&](int st) {
        const uint8_t * sw = sm + (st % NSH) * STG;
        const int p = st & 3;
        constexpr float OFF = TYPE == TQ6K ? -1056.f : TYPE == TQ80 ? -1152.f : -1024.f;
        const __half2 off2 = __float2half2_rn(OFF);
#pragma unroll
        for (int mi = 0; mi < HMI; mi++) {
        const int rw = HWR * wm + 16 * mi + g;
        const uint8_t * ra = sw + rw * SR;
        const uint8_t * rb = ra + 8 * SR;
        // weight words: wd[j][kk][r] = 4 values of elements 16 kk + 4 t4 .. of sub-block j, row g + 8 r
        unsigned wd[2][2][2];
        if constexpr (TYPE == TQ4K || TYPE == TQ5K) {
            constexpr int QO = TYPE == TQ4K ? 16 : 48;
            const unsigned w0 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(ra + QO + 4 * t4);
            const unsigned w1 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(ra + QO + 16 + 4 * t4);
            const unsigned w2 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(rb + QO + 4 * t4);
            const unsigned w3 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(rb + QO + 16 + 4 * t4);
            wd[0][0][0] = w0 & M4; wd[0][0][1] = w2 & M4; wd[0][1][0] = w1 & M4; wd[0][1][1] = w3 & M4;
            wd[1][0][0] = (w0 >> 4) & M4; wd[1][0][1] = (w2 >> 4) & M4;
            wd[1][1][0] = (w1 >> 4) & M4; wd[1][1][1] = (w3 >> 4) & M4;
            if constexpr (TYPE == TQ5K) {
                const unsigned h0 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(ra + 16 + 4 * t4) >> (2 * p);
                const unsigned h1 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(ra + 32 + 4 * t4) >> (2 * p);
                const unsigned h2 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(rb + 16 + 4 * t4) >> (2 * p);
                const unsigned h3 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(rb + 32 + 4 * t4) >> (2 * p);
                wd[0][0][0] |= (h0 & 0x01010101u) << 4; wd[0][0][1] |= (h2 & 0x01010101u) << 4;
                wd[0][1][0] |= (h1 & 0x01010101u) << 4; wd[0][1][1] |= (h3 & 0x01010101u) << 4;
                wd[1][0][0] |= (h0 & 0x02020202u) << 3; wd[1][0][1] |= (h2 & 0x02020202u) << 3;
                wd[1][1][0] |= (h1 & 0x02020202u) << 3; wd[1][1][1] |= (h3 & 0x02020202u) << 3;
            }
        } else if constexpr (TYPE == TQ6K) {
            const int z = (g >> 1) & 3; // rows g and g + 8 share the swizzle
            const unsigned w0 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(ra + 16 * z + 4 * t4);
            const unsigned w1 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(ra + 16 * (1 ^ z) + 4 * t4);
            const unsigned w2 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(rb + 16 * z + 4 * t4);
            const unsigned w3 = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(rb + 16 * (1 ^ z) + 4 * t4);
            const uint4 ha = *reinterpret_cast<const uint4 *>(ra + 16 * (2 ^ z));
            const uint4 hb = *reinterpret_cast<const uint4 *>(rb + 16 * (2 ^ z));
            const int s = 2 * t4;
            auto mk = [](unsigned lo4, unsigned hi2) { return lo4 | ((hi2 & 0x03030303u) << 4); }; // 0..63
            wd[0][0][0] = mk(w0 & M4, ha.x >> s);
            wd[0][0][1] = mk(w2 & M4, hb.x >> s);
            wd[0][1][0] = mk(w1 & M4, ha.z >> s);
            wd[0][1][1] = mk(w3 & M4, hb.z >> s);
            wd[1][0][0] = mk((w0 >> 4) & M4, ha.y >> s);
            wd[1][0][1] = mk((w2 >> 4) & M4, hb.y >> s);
            wd[1][1][0] = mk((w1 >> 4) & M4, ha.w >> s);
            wd[1][1][1] = mk((w3 >> 4) & M4, hb.w >> s);
        } else {
#pragma unroll
            for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++)
#pragma unroll
                for (int kk = 0; kk < 2; kk++) {
                    wd[j][kk][0] = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(ra + 16 * j + 32 * kk + 4 * t4) ^ 0x80808080u;
                    wd[j][kk][1] = *reinterpret_cast<const unsigned *>(rb + 16 * j + 32 * kk + 4 * t4) ^ 0x80808080u;
                }
        }
#pragma unroll
        for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++)
#pragma unroll
            for (int kk = 0; kk < 2; kk++)
#pragma unroll
                for (int r = 0; r < 2; r++)
#pragma unroll
                    for (int hh = 0; hh < 2; hh++)
                        vq[mi][j][kk][2 * hh + r] =
                            h2u(__hadd2(u2h(__byte_perm(wd[j][kk][r], 0x64646464u, hh ? 0x4342 : 0x4140)), off2));
        }
    };
    unsigned ch[HMI][HNI][2];
    auto step = [&](int st, auto ph) {
        constexpr int PH = decltype(ph)::value;
        cpa_wait<NSH - 3>();
        __syncthreads();

        const uint8_t * sw = sm + (st % NSH) * STG;
        const unsigned sx = sbase + (st % NSH) * STG;
        const uint2 * ws = sws + (st & 1) * 2 * BM;
        const int p = st & 3;
        rawq(st);
        unsigned a[HMI][2][2][4]; // fp16 A fragments [mi][j][kk][0..3]
#pragma unroll
        for (int mi = 0; mi < HMI; mi++) {
        const int rw = HWR * wm + 16 * mi + g;
        const uint8_t * ra = sw + rw * SR;
        const uint8_t * rb = ra + 8 * SR;
#pragma unroll
        for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
            uint2 sA, sB;
            if constexpr (HT<TYPE>::SEP) {
                sA = ws[j * BM + rw];
                sB = ws[j * BM + rw + 8];
            } else { // Q6_K: (S_j0, S_j1) in the row -> broadcast pairs
                const int z = (g >> 1) & 3;
                const __half2 qa = *reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(ra + 16 * (3 ^ z) + 8 + 4 * j);
                const __half2 qb = *reinterpret_cast<const __half2 *>(rb + 16 * (3 ^ z) + 8 + 4 * j);
                sA = make_uint2(h2u(__low2half2(qa)), h2u(__high2half2(qa)));
                sB = make_uint2(h2u(__low2half2(qb)), h2u(__high2half2(qb)));
            }
#pragma unroll
            for (int kk = 0; kk < 2; kk++)
#pragma unroll
                for (int r = 0; r < 2; r++) {
                    const uint2 sc = r ? sB : sA;
#pragma unroll
                    for (int hh = 0; hh < 2; hh++) {
                        const __half2 v = u2h(vq[mi][j][kk][2 * hh + r]);
                        __half2 o;
                        if constexpr (TYPE == TQ4K || TYPE == TQ5K) o = __hfma2(v, u2h(sc.x), u2h(sc.y));
                        else if constexpr (TYPE == TQ6K) o = __hmul2(v, u2h(kk ? sc.y : sc.x));
                        else o = __hmul2(v, u2h(sc.x));
                        a[mi][j][kk][2 * hh + r] = h2u(o);
                    }
                }
        }
        }
#pragma unroll
        for (int G = 0; G < 4; G++) {
            const int j = G >> 1, kk = G & 1;
#pragma unroll
            for (int np = 0; np < HNI / 2; np++) {
                unsigned r[4];
                ldsm_x4(r, sx + xo[G] + np * 16 * 128);
#pragma unroll
                for (int mi = 0; mi < HMI; mi++) {
                    if (G == 0 && (PH == 0 || HCH == 1)) {
                        hmma16z(ch[mi][2 * np], a[mi][j][kk], r[0], r[1]);
                        hmma16z(ch[mi][2 * np + 1], a[mi][j][kk], r[2], r[3]);
                    } else {
                        hmma16(ch[mi][2 * np], a[mi][j][kk], r[0], r[1]);
                        hmma16(ch[mi][2 * np + 1], a[mi][j][kk], r[2], r[3]);
                    }
                }
            }
            // next stages' copies and scales are issued once the tensor pipe has work queued
            if (G == 0) {
                if (st + NSH - 1 < nst) load(st + NSH - 1);
                cpa_commit();
            }
            if (G == 1 && st + 1 < nst) scales(st + 1);
        }
        if (PH == HCH - 1 || HCH == 1)
#pragma unroll
        for (int mi = 0; mi < HMI; mi++)
#pragma unroll
        for (int ni = 0; ni < HNI; ni++) {
            const float2 lo = __half22float2(u2h(ch[mi][ni][0])), hi = __half22float2(u2h(ch[mi][ni][1]));
            acc[mi][ni][0] += lo.x; acc[mi][ni][1] += lo.y; acc[mi][ni][2] += hi.x; acc[mi][ni][3] += hi.y;
        }
    };
    for (int st = 0; st < nst; st += 2) { // nst is even for every K of the model
        step(st, std::integral_constant<int, 0>());
        step(st + 1, std::integral_constant<int, 1>());
    }
    cpa_wait<0>();
    __syncthreads();
    constexpr int ELD = BM + 4;
    float * se = reinterpret_cast<float *>(sm);
#pragma unroll
    for (int mi = 0; mi < HMI; mi++)
#pragma unroll
    for (int ni = 0; ni < HNI; ni++) {
        const int r = HWR * wm + 16 * mi + g, tk = HWT * wn + 8 * ni + 2 * t4;
        se[tk * ELD + r] = acc[mi][ni][0];
        se[(tk + 1) * ELD + r] = acc[mi][ni][1];
        se[tk * ELD + r + 8] = acc[mi][ni][2];
        se[(tk + 1) * ELD + r + 8] = acc[mi][ni][3];
    }
    __syncthreads();
    if constexpr (GLU) {
        for (int i = tid; i < BN * 4; i += 256) { // (token, 16-group of the 64 outputs)
            const int tk = i >> 2, q = i & 3;
            if (tok0 + tk >= ntok) continue;
            const float * gp = se + tk * ELD + 16 * q;
            __align__(16) __half hv[16];
#pragma unroll
            for (int e = 0; e < 16; e++) {
                const float gv = gp[e], v = gv / (1.0f + expf(-gv)) * gp[e + BM / 2];
                hv[hperm(e)] = __float2half_rn(fminf(fmaxf(v, -65504.f), 65504.f));
            }
            uint4 * dst = reinterpret_cast<uint4 *>(O.yh + (size_t) (tok0 + tk) * O.ldy + row0 + 16 * q);
            dst[0] = reinterpret_cast<const uint4 *>(hv)[0];
            dst[1] = reinterpret_cast<const uint4 *>(hv)[1];
        }
        return;
    }
    for (int i = tid; i < BN * BM / 4; i += 256) {
        const int tk = i / (BM / 4), c = (i % (BM / 4)) * 4;
        if (tok0 + tk >= ntok) continue;
        float4 v = *reinterpret_cast<const float4 *>(se + tk * ELD + c);
        float4 * dst = reinterpret_cast<float4 *>(O.y + (size_t) (tok0 + tk) * O.ldy + row0 + c);
        if (O.mode == OUT_ADD) {
            const float4 o = __ldcg(dst);
            v.x += o.x; v.y += o.y; v.z += o.z; v.w += o.w;
        }
        *dst = v;
    }
}

} // namespace pf

END FILE

FILE: megakernel/tests/external_verify.cpp
#include "../qwen38_mk.h"
#include "../gguf.h"
#include "ggml.h"
#include "ggml-backend.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <fstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

static void require(bool ok,const char * message) { if (!ok) throw std::runtime_error(message); }
static std::vector<int> read_ids(const char * path) {
    std::ifstream f(path);std::vector<int> v;int id;
    while (f>>id) v.push_back(id);
    require(f.eof() && !v.empty(),"invalid prompt IDs");return v;
}

int main(int argc,char ** argv) {
    try {
        require(argc==4 || argc==5,"usage: external_verify_test model short.ids long.ids [kv_mode 0|1|2]");
        const int kv_mode = argc==5 ? std::stoi(argv[4]) : 1;
        setenv("MK_PREFILL_CHUNK","128",1);
        auto prompt=read_ids(argv[2]);
        gguf::File file(argv[1]);
        MKEngine engine(argv[1],4096,0,"",kv_mode);
        std::vector<int> reference{engine.prefill(prompt)};
        while (reference.size()<80) engine.step(reference);
        engine.reset();
        auto weights=engine.share_weights();
        require(engine.share_weights().output==weights.output,"shared weight identity changed");
        for (auto entry:{std::pair<ggml_tensor *,const char *>{weights.embedding,"token_embd.weight"},
                        {weights.output,"output.weight"}}) {
            const auto & original=file.get(entry.second);
            require(ggml_nbytes(entry.first)==original.nbytes,"shared weight shape changed");
            std::vector<uint8_t> bytes(16384);
            for (size_t offset:{size_t(0),size_t(original.nbytes/2),size_t(original.nbytes-bytes.size())}) {
                ggml_backend_tensor_get(entry.first,bytes.data(),offset,bytes.size());
                require(!std::memcmp(bytes.data(),original.data+offset,bytes.size()),"shared weights changed bytes");
            }
        }
        std::vector<int> expected=prompt;
        expected.insert(expected.end(),reference.begin(),reference.end());
        int next_position=0,callbacks=0;
        engine.enable_features({0,6,20,34,48,62},[&](int pos,int n,const std::vector<float> & features) {
            require(pos==next_position,"feature positions are not contiguous");
            require(features.size()==size_t(n)*6*5120,"feature shape mismatch");
            require(pos+n<=int(expected.size()),"feature position outside known sequence");
            for (float v:features) require(std::isfinite(v),"non-finite feature");
            std::vector<float> embedding(5120);
            const auto & tensor=file.get("token_embd.weight");
            for (int t=0;t<n;t++) {
                ggml_get_type_traits(ggml_type(tensor.type))->to_float(
                    tensor.data+size_t(expected[pos+t])*gguf::type_row_bytes(tensor.type,5120),embedding.data(),5120);
                for (int e=0;e<5120;e++) if(std::abs(embedding[e]-features[size_t(t)*6*5120+e])>1e-6f) {
                    fprintf(stderr,"feature mismatch pos=%d count=%d token=%d e=%d expected=%g actual=%g\n",
                        pos+t,n,expected[pos+t],e,embedding[e],features[size_t(t)*6*5120+e]);
                    throw std::runtime_error("layer-zero feature does not match CPU embedding");
                }
            }
            next_position+=n;callbacks++;
        });
        for (int depth:{0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7}) {
        for (int rejected=0;rejected<=depth;rejected++) {
            engine.reset();next_position=0;
            std::vector<int> got{engine.prefill(prompt)};
            std::vector<int> proposals(reference.begin()+1,reference.begin()+1+depth);
            if (rejected<depth) proposals[rejected]=(proposals[rejected]+1)%engine.vocab_size();
            engine.verify_external(proposals,got);
            require(engine.stats.accepted==rejected,"unexpected first rejection position");
            require(got.size()==size_t(rejected+2),"incorrect accepted prefix plus bonus");
            while (got.size()<64) {
                proposals.assign(reference.begin()+got.size(),reference.begin()+got.size()+depth);
                engine.verify_external(proposals,got);
            }
            require(std::equal(got.begin(),got.begin()+64,reference.begin()),"external verifier differs from scalar decode");
            require(engine.position()==int(engine.sequence().size())-1,"sequence position mismatch");
            require(engine.committed()<=engine.position(),"recurrent state committed speculative rows");
            printf("PASS depth %d rejection %d, scalar IDs and continued replay\n",depth,rejected);
        }
        }
        const auto before=engine.sequence();
        for (auto invalid:{std::vector<int>{-1},std::vector<int>{engine.vocab_size()},std::vector<int>(8,0)}) {
            bool rejected=false;std::vector<int> out;
            try { engine.verify_external(invalid,out); } catch (const std::runtime_error &) { rejected=true; }
            require(rejected && engine.sequence()==before,"invalid proposal mutated sequence");
        }
        engine.reset();next_position=0;
        expected=read_ids(argv[3]);
        engine.prefill(expected);
        require(next_position==int(expected.size()),"chunked prefill features skipped positions");
        require(expected.size()>128,"long prompt did not cross prefill chunk");
        printf("PASS: shared IQ2_S/Q4_K bytes, invalid proposals, %d feature callbacks and long chunked prefill\n",callbacks);
    } catch (const std::exception & e) { fprintf(stderr,"FAIL: %s\n",e.what());return 1; }
    return 0;
}

END FILE
